square holds but qL_f†=2≠1=L_c†p; the update difference is −η qx.

For invertible domain/codomain charts S,T, transport
G_V'=S^(−*)G_VS⁻¹, G_W'=T^(−*)G_WT⁻¹, L'=TLS⁻¹, b'=Tb.
Using L†=G_V⁻¹L*G_W, multiplication gives L'†=SL†T⁻¹. Thus the gradient
and update transport exactly by S, with the same intrinsic spectrum.

NGD constructs a concrete finite label-cover decoder D and section E
with DE=I. D adds coefficients for occurrences of the same literal source
label; E chooses its first declared occurrence. On an actual original Gram
form G and lag form H, the retained cover forms are

 Ghat=D*GD, Hhat=D*HD, P=ED,
 E*Ghat E=G, E*Hhat E=H,
 P*Ghat=Ghat=Ghat P, P*Hhat=Hhat=Hhat P.

For every real q, qG−H≥0 iff qGhat−Hhat≥0: evaluate the original form on
Dv in one direction and the pulled-back form on Eu in the other. The full
ker D is a null space of both pulled-back forms. This is exact overlap
gluing with all original labels, not an arbitrary positive extension.
The source proof keeps any later infinite-word completion and physical
semigroup hypotheses separate from this finite matrix theorem.

14. NEW INTEGRAL FOURIER DESCENT WITH EXACT WHOLE-SOURCE RETURN

Use the concrete recovered maps of section 3, G=U†:O₂²→O₂³, L=U, so LG=I.
Let y∈O₂³ have coordinate sum zero and retain its actual source record.
Then u_*=Uy and Gu_*=y. For any u₀∈O₂² and any integer s≥1 define

 u_(n+1)=u_n+(1+2^s)U(y−U†u_n).

Linearity and UU†=I give, without an inequality or omitted term,

 e_(n+1)=u_(n+1)−u_*=−2^s(u_n−u_*)=−2^s e_n.

Induction yields e_n=(−2^s)^n e₀. In the maximum coordinate valuation on
O₂², multiplication by 2^s increases the valuation exactly by s for
nonzero error. Completeness gives u_n→u_*. After ceil(h/s) steps every
integral initial error vanishes modulo 2^h. Applying integral U† returns
the original full y modulo the same cut. Amplitude analyzers then read its
original X,Y; any attached proof witness and grade remain part of the packet.

This supplies an explicit polynomial finite-cut implementation. At h bits,
the fixed 2×3 CM matrices require a constant number of additions, sign
changes, shifts and exact modular divisions by 3. To compute a/3 modulo
2^h, choose k∈{0,1,2} making a+k2^h divisible by 3, then perform ordinary
binary division by 3; the residue selection and division each cost O(h).
CM multiplication by ω is the fixed integer map (x,y)↦(−y,x−y).
Each update therefore costs O(h) elementary bit operations and O(h)
working bits, with total O(h ceil(h/s)+h). The source y at precision h
must be supplied or its construction cost added. This is a costed exact
decoder for the specified source plane, with no hidden input producer.

The theorem is a new composition of recovered U and U†. Its literal CASL
functions are cmsDescentStep, cmsDescentError and cmsReconstructedSource
in formal/Integral_Fourier_Descent.het. The equational theorem works with
an arbitrary scalar t; the contraction interpretation sets t=2^s.

15. INTEGRAL q-CALCULUS AND HYPERBOLIC CLIFFORD SPECIALIZATION [QR]

Over Z[q], let [0]_q=0, [n+1]_q=1+q[n]_q. Multiplication X by x and
D_q(x^n)=[n]_q x^(n−1) satisfy, on every monomial,
(D_qX−qXD_q)x^n=([n+1]_q−q[n]_q)x^n=x^n. Linearity proves the operator
identity on all polynomials. At q=1 this is ordinary differential calculus.
At q=2, all 2^n−1 for n>0 are odd units, so

 J₂(Σb_nx^n)=Σ b_n x^(n+1)/(2^(n+1)−1)

is an integral x-adically continuous antiderivative over Z₂, with
D₂J₂=I and J₂D₂f=f−f(0), coefficient by coefficient.

At q=−1 the ideal (x²) is stable. On basis (1,x),
C=[[0,0],[1,0]], D=[[0,1],[0,0]], Z=I−2CD.
Then C²=D²=0, DC+CD=I and (aC+bD)²=abI. These are integral
hyperbolic Clifford relations, with no inverse of 2. For two modes,
C₁=C⊗I,D₁=D⊗I,C₂=Z⊗C,D₂=Z⊗D. The diagonal Z anticommutes with C,D;
combining that fact with the one-mode relations proves
{C_i,C_j}={D_i,D_j}=0, {D_i,C_j}=δ_ijI. The carrier is the rank-four
exterior module. Over C with its standard positive pairing C_i†=D_i.

The formal specialization uses the polynomial q-Weyl law. It does not
specialize a denominator 1−q^(2n) that vanishes at q=−1. The full recovered
QR source additionally proves its bounded-operator and dyadic filters,
with the convergence, invertibility and original-intertwiner hypotheses
stated explicitly there.

16. EXACT VERIFICATION AND THE FORMAL SOURCE BOUNDARY

check_exact.py verifies 353 exact symbolic or integral matrix statements:
the CM norm, Gram determinant and prefix update; all Fourier/dagger/source
readbacks and marked analyzers; mixed determinants; the new error equation;
the Cl(3,1) relations, sixteen-blade Gram and signed basis products; exact
Pauli frame phases; biquaternion norms; Hopf sections and overlap; integral
fermion relations; all 81-cell tesseract contraction matrices; and the
two-sided Clifford adjoint and nonunitary metric covariance. It also checks
the explicit failure when the adjoint square is omitted. No floating-point
estimate or source-orbit enumeration is used.

The full universal statements follow from the displayed polynomial,
generator, linearity or induction proofs; finite-cut certificates are not
substitutes for any unprovided infinite analytic premise. The recovered
Hets %implied labels are indexed in spec_roots.json. A %implied declaration
identifies a goal; any new native prover receipt should be attached
separately. This branch neither assumes a new minimum oracle nor identifies
a supplied finite carrier with an unrestricted global conjecture.

17. DIRECT OCTOBER 15 DESCENT ORIGINALS AND THEIR TEST SCOPE

The exact newly retrieved originals are copied, with hashes, under
sources/original_descent/. The proof note is
clifford-descent-predictions-and-proofs.md; the separate evidence note is
clifford-descent-independent-test-results.md. Their embedded Python programs
are extracted literally under recovered_code/. The main note's section 3
contains exactly the three equations (13.1) and the substitution proof
qD_f(x)=qx−ηL_c†(pL_fx−pb_f)=D_c(qx). Its own forward-only counterexample is
L_f=[[1,0],[1,1]], p=q=[1,0], L_c=1, x=(0,1), b_f=0. Then pL_f=L_cq,
but qL_fᵀL_fx=1 while L_cᵀL_cqx=0. This supplements the different explicit
counterexample in section 13; both retain the missing adjoint information.

The sibling matrix branch now has fresh E2.5 proofs of the typed gradient
and update squares, recorded in ../matrix/atp/atp_receipt.json, with literal
CASL source ../matrix/Frame_Descent_Covariance.het. This local proof receipt
is separate from the October note's finite tests and analytic statements.

Two further constructors in that original are completely explicit. For
wave coordinates (b,a), the map L=[1,0], target zero, gives D₁(b,a)=(0,a).
With a=(V+Z₀I)/2 and b=(V−Z₀I)/2, it retains the incident amplitude and
sets reflection to zero. The ratio b/a is used only on a≠0; the full
amplitude pair retains the excluded chart boundary. For the triangle,

 d=[[-1,1,0],[0,-1,1],[1,0,-1]], ddᵀ=3I−11ᵀ,
 x'=(I−ddᵀ/3)x=((x₁+x₂+x₃)/3)1,
 x'−x=d(−dᵀx/3).

Multiplication proves harmonicity and the same loop period. This is a
full exact theorem for that graph and metric. The weighted triangle and
the square require their own Laplacians, as the independent controls show.

For the native controller C=[[1,1],[-1,0]], M=I−2C=[[-1,-2],[2,1]],
M⁻¹=(1/3)[[1,2],[-2,-1]], C³=−I and (2C)⁶=64I. Thus x_*=M⁻¹b is
defined over Z₂, e_(n+6)=64e_n and the vector error valuation gains six.
For the real positive Gram [[2,1],[1,2]], CᵀGC=G, so the same 2C expands
the real norm by two each step. Both readings retain their actual metric.

The original five-qubit code uses XZZXI, IXZZX, XIXZZ, ZXIXZ and an explicit
encoding V:C²→C³². With its sixteen signed error representatives E_s and
V_s=E_sV, the supplied exact code calculation gives V_s†V_t=δ_stI and
ΣV_sV_s†=I. Therefore U=(V₀ ... V₁₅) is unitary. Define
Lhat=U(I₁₆⊗L)U†. Multiplying by each V_s gives both
Lhat V_s=V_s L and Lhat†V_s=V_s L†. Consequently the logical descent
commutes with every retained syndrome embedding, and U† returns the
syndrome and the same logical iterate. General physical operators retain
all 16×16 logical blocks, including the off-diagonal ones. This is the
specific supplied error model and code, not an assertion about arbitrary
uncorrectable errors.

The finite matching witness uses the actual two-field Elias-gamma parser.
The packet gamma(2)gamma(1)=0101 satisfies 4(2−1)²≤(2+1)². Its least-
significant-first dyadic address is 10 modulo16. Because the parser stops
after exactly these two fields, every tail returns the same proposition:
the proof ball is exactly 10+16Z₂ for this fixed record. This stopping proof
is universal in the tail; the source test of 256 eight-bit tails is a finite
exercise of that theorem, not its proof for arbitrary length.

The independent evidence note is retained as historical evidence, not
described as rerun here. Its 1,920 finite-difference directions, 100
rank-deficient simulations, 625 wave fixtures, 100 metric changes, 729
triangle vectors, 27 compatible-map cases, code/channel simulations,
87,380 dyadic right-hand sides and 131,071 parser inputs are specified
finite domains. Derivative, gauge and channel results use double precision;
the integer, rational, modular and parser statements use exact arithmetic.
Its failure controls detect oversized step length, forgotten adjoint data,
changed graphs/metrics, nonlinear Euler-versus-conjugation discrepancy,
weight-two code errors and nonlinear stationary residuals. Those controls
sharpen the hypotheses of the mathematical proofs; they do not replace them.
9. Filtered minimal comparison complexes and homological perturbation with original return

Complete original source: Hilbert24_Constructive_Use.zip :: Hilbert24_Constructive_Use/Filtered_Minimal_Comparison_Proofs.txt

Finite-free chain complexes over Z_2, or the declared complete separated local-ring generalization, with explicit chain maps i,p and homotopy h satisfying pi=1, 1−ip=dh+hd and h²=hi=ph=0. Terminating unit cancellation requires a bounded finite-free input; supplied contractions permit the other identities degreewise. Minimality compares all finite-free complexes with supplied homotopy equivalence and retained differential zero over the residue field. Filtered perturbation requires δ in the maximal ideal and (d+δ)²=0. Every returned original filler retains its reduced witness and homotopy correction.

§3 Global degreewise minimum (5)–(6); §4 Perturbation theorem (9)–(12); §5 Minimality of the deformed family; §6 Original and higher comparison return (14)–(15); §7 Minimal-model chain isomorphism (16); §8 Two-sided Newton residual squaring (17)–(18)

FILTERED MINIMAL COMPARISON COMPLEXES
Constructive derivations for the Hilbert-24 continuation
18 November 2026

Status and purpose
------------------
These are written mathematical proofs, not a claim of fresh Hets replay. The
homological perturbation lemma and minimal-complex arguments are established
mathematics; their role here is to extend the recovered Hilbert-24 machinery
from comparing a fixed collection of syzygies to constructing and maintaining
minimal comparison presentations under an entire filtered deformation.

Everything below is constructive from the displayed data. The operations are
finite matrix arithmetic, inversion of units, primitive recursion, and the
definition of an inverse limit by compatible residues. No selection among an
unprovided family of witnesses, axiom of choice, or enumeration of competing
proofs occurs.

The exact object being minimized is a finite-free comparison complex: degree
zero may encode marked proof data, degree one admitted comparisons, degree two
comparisons of comparisons, and so on. Its interpretation in the original
proof language is part of the supplied source packet. Linearization by itself
does not establish that every equality of arbitrary proofs is represented.

1. Data and sign convention
--------------------------
Put R = Z_2. More generally the same construction works over a complete
separated local ring R with maximal ideal m and residue field k, replacing
2-adic powers by m-adic powers. For an effective concrete instance, use the
given finite bases and effective residue operations. R = Z_2[[a]] with
m = (2,a) is another relevant instance.

Let (C,d) and (K,D) be chain complexes of finite-free R-modules in each degree.
Differentials lower degree by one. We are given maps

  i: K -> C,   p: C -> K,   h: C_n -> C_(n+1)

satisfying

  d^2 = 0, D^2 = 0, di = iD, pd = Dp,
  pi = 1,
  1 - ip = dh + hd,
  h^2 = 0, hi = 0, ph = 0.                         (1)

These are strong deformation-retraction data with the explicitly stated sign
convention. Other references use ip = 1 + dh + hd; their h is the negative of
ours. All formulas below use (1).

Boundedness of C is not needed for the identities, the minimality theorem, or
the uniqueness theorem when the data (1) are supplied. The terminating unit
cancellation algorithm in Section 2 applies to a bounded finite-free complex.
An unbounded algorithm needs a separately supplied locally finite elimination
schedule; no unrestricted infinite cancellation procedure is asserted here.

2. Constructing the contraction by cancelling units
--------------------------------------------------
Suppose a displayed matrix for d_n contains a unit. Permute its row and column
to obtain a decomposition C_n = X + Rx, C_(n-1) = Y + Ry with

                  [ a  b ]
  d_n =           [ c  u ],     u a unit.

Here '+' denotes a direct sum. Set

                  [ I      0 ]             [ I  -b u^(-1) ]
  V =             [ -u^-1 c 1 ],   U =      [ 0    u^(-1)  ].

Both matrices have explicit triangular inverses, and direct multiplication
gives

  U d_n V = diag(a - b u^(-1)c, 1).                (2)

Change the neighbouring differentials to V^(-1)d_(n+1) and d_(n-1)U^(-1).
The equation d_n d_(n+1)=0 forces the x-component of the first neighbour to
vanish after this change. The equation d_(n-1)d_n=0 forces the second neighbour
to vanish on y. Thus Rx -> Ry with differential 1 is an isolated direct
summand of the entire complex. Remove it. The retained differential is the
Schur expression a - b u^(-1)c in degree n.

In the changed coordinates, inclusion i and projection p are literal direct
summand maps. Define h(y)=x and h=0 on the other basis summands. The two
equations dh(x)=0, hd(x)=x and dh(y)=y, hd(y)=0 verify
1-ip=dh+hd on the removed disk; both sides vanish on the retained complex.
The side identities pi=1, h^2=hi=ph=0 are immediate. Conjugating back by U,V
gives the contraction in the original marked coordinates.

Over Z_2, detecting a unit means reading its residue modulo 2. Inverting it
uses the compatible inverse constructed in Section 7. Cancelling a pivot 2^e
with e>0 is not licensed: the associated torsion remains in K.

Choose the first available unit in the declared finite row and column order,
apply (2), and repeat. Every cancellation decreases the total module rank by
two, so this terminates for a bounded finite-free complex. At termination all
entries of D lie in 2R, hence

  D mod 2 = 0.                                   (3)

The finite scan of displayed matrix entries is a linear-algebra operation on
the input presentation, not enumeration of the family of proofs being
compared.

Contractions compose without losing their return witnesses. If C retracts to
K by (i_1,p_1,h_1) and K retracts to L by (i_2,p_2,h_2), take

  i = i_1 i_2,   p = p_2 p_1,
  h = h_1 + i_1 h_2 p_1.                         (4)

Indeed

  dh+hd = (1-i_1p_1) + i_1(1-i_2p_2)p_1
        = 1 - i_1i_2p_2p_1.

The cross terms in h^2 vanish by h_1i_1=p_1h_1=0, and the remaining term is
i_1 h_2^2 p_1=0. The identities hi=ph=0 follow in the same way. Thus (4)
retains a single explicit homotopy for the whole elimination history.

3. Global minimality in every comparison degree
-----------------------------------------------
THEOREM. Suppose (K,D) satisfies (3) and has finite-free modules. For every
finite-free complex E equipped with a chain homotopy equivalence to K,

  rank_R K_n <= rank_R E_n                       (5)

in every degree n simultaneously.

PROOF. Reduce the supplied chain maps and chain homotopies modulo 2.
Polynomial equalities defining a chain homotopy equivalence remain equalities
after reduction; no exactness assumption about tensoring is needed. We get a
chain homotopy equivalence of complexes of finite-dimensional F_2-vector
spaces. Since D mod 2=0,

  H_n(K/2K) = K_n/2K_n.

Therefore

  rank_R K_n = dim_F2 H_n(K/2K)
             = dim_F2 H_n(E/2E)
             <= dim_F2(E_n/2E_n)
             = rank_R E_n.

The dimension inequality concerns a subquotient of a finite-dimensional vector
space and has the usual finite elimination proof. This proves (5). END.

If C retracts to K, the attained lower bound can be read on the original cut:

  beta_n = dim_F2 H_n(C/2C)
         = rank_R C_n - rank_F2(d_n mod 2)
                       - rank_F2(d_(n+1) mod 2)
         = rank_R K_n.                          (6)

Thus the residue complex supplies a certificate against every competing
finite-free homotopy-equivalent presentation, while unit cancellation supplies
the attaining presentation. This is a simultaneous minimum of the complete
vector of generator counts, not merely a minimum for one scalar weighting.
It is a precise Hilbert-24 simplicity result for this representation class.

The proof works over a complete local ring using the residue field k. The
completeness is needed for the explicit inverse constructions, not for the
dimension inequality (5).

4. Correcting the reduction when the comparison rules change
-----------------------------------------------------------
Let delta:C_n -> C_(n-1) preserve the R-module structure, have coefficients in
2R, and satisfy

  (d+delta)^2=0,
  equivalently d delta + delta d + delta^2=0.     (7)

It may change the retained differential and actual integral homology. It is
not assumed to leave the comparison problem unchanged. Its effect is retained
explicitly by the construction below.

Because h delta raises the 2-adic filtration, the operator

  (1+h delta)^(-1) = sum_(j>=0) (-h delta)^j      (8)

is defined by its compatible finite residues. Modulo 2^m, terms j>=m vanish.
Multiplication of the finite sum by 1+h delta gives
1-(-h delta)^m, equal to 1 modulo 2^m. Thus it is a two-sided inverse at every
cut, and the inverses commute with reduction. This proves (8) directly, without
using a general existence theorem for analytic contraction.

Put

  A  = delta (1+h delta)^(-1)
     = (1+delta h)^(-1) delta,
  D' = D + p A i,
  i' = i - h A i,
  p' = p - p A h,
  h' = h - h A h,
  d' = d + delta.                                (9)

The equality defining A follows from
(1+delta h)delta=delta(1+h delta) and the already constructed inverses.

THEOREM. The maps in (9) satisfy

  D'^2=0, d'i'=i'D', p'd'=D'p',
  p'i'=1, 1-i'p'=d'h'+h'd',
  h'^2=h'i'=p'h'=0.                             (10)

PROOF, WITH THE ALGEBRAIC CANCELLATIONS. Write L=1+delta h, R_0=1+h delta.
The inverse equations imply

  A=delta-delta h A=delta-A h delta.             (11)

The main identity is

  dA + Ad + AipA = 0.                            (12)

Multiply its left side by L on the left and R_0 on the right. Using LA=delta,
AR_0=delta, and dh+hd+ip=1, the result is

  L d delta + delta d R_0 + delta ip delta
  = d delta + delta d + delta(hd+dh+ip)delta
  = d delta + delta d + delta^2
  = 0.

Both multipliers are invertible, so (12) follows.

Now

  D'^2 = p(dA+Ad+AipA)i = 0.

Expanding d'i'-i'D', using di=iD, and substituting (11), gives

  d'i'-i'D'
  = Ai - dhAi - ipAi + hAdi + hAipAi
  = h(dA+Ad+AipA)i
  = 0.

Expanding p'd'-D'p' similarly gives

  p'd'-D'p'
  = pA - pAhd - pAip + pdAh + pAipAh
  = p(Ad+dA+AipA)h
  = 0.

The product p'i' equals

  pi - phAi - pAhi + pAhhAi = 1

by the side identities in (1). For the homotopy equation, expand and combine
the delta terms using (11):

  d'h'+h'd'
  = dh+hd + Ah+hA - dhAh-hAhd
  = 1-ip + ipAh+hAip + h(dA+Ad)h
  = 1-ip + ipAh+hAip - hAipAh
  = 1-i'p'.

Finally every term in h'^2, h'i' and p'h' contains one of h^2, hi, or ph,
after expansion. All vanish. This proves (10). END.

All displayed arguments apply separately in each finite quotient R/2^mR.
Their compatibility gives a single inverse-limit construction. The arbitrary
depth is a parameter of the construction, not a list of numerical cases.

5. Minimality of the entire deformed family
------------------------------------------
If the original retained differential satisfies D mod 2=0, then A has entries
in 2R because delta does. Hence

  D' mod 2 = 0.                                 (13)

Apply Section 3 to (K,D'). It has the same underlying free modules as K and is
minimal in every degree among finite-free complexes homotopy equivalent to
(C,d+delta). Equivalently, d+delta and d have the same residue differential,
so their numbers beta_n from (6) agree. The filtered correction changes the
integral differential while retaining a presentation with the least possible
number of free generators in each degree.

This does not assert that integral homology stays fixed. For example R --0--> R
and R --2--> R both have minimal rank vector (1,1), while their homology
differs. The exact entry 2 is the information that a legitimate reduction must
retain. Formula (9) retains such entries.

6. Returning every comparison and every higher comparison
--------------------------------------------------------
Let b in C_n satisfy d'b=0. To solve the marked source comparison

  d'x = b,

solve the smaller retained problem

  D'y = p'b.

Given such a y, return

  G_b(y) = i'y + h'b.                            (14)

Indeed, by (10),

  d'G_b(y) = i'D'y+d'h'b
            = i'p'b + (1-i'p'-h'd')b
            = b.

Projection F_b(x)=p'x sends any source solution to a retained solution. The
return identities are

  F_b(G_b(y)) = y,
  G_b(F_b(x)) = x - d'h'x.                       (15)

The first uses p'i'=1 and p'h'=0. The second uses d'x=b and the homotopy
identity. Thus the loss under reduction is an explicit higher comparison,
namely h'x, rather than an unrecorded identification.

If x,x' are source comparisons and z in K_(n+2) satisfies

  D'z = p'(x'-x),

then

  i'z + h'(x'-x)

has boundary x'-x, because d'(x'-x)=0. Repeating the same formula in higher
degrees returns comparisons of comparisons at every specified level.

Equation (14) is the direct operational use of proof transport. The original
source discrepancy b stays fixed, the reduced equation contains its exact
image, and the returned witness includes its homotopy correction.

7. Unique minimal models up to a constructible gauge isomorphism
---------------------------------------------------------------
THEOREM. Let (K,D_K) and (J,D_J) be degreewise finite-free minimal complexes,
meaning D_K mod 2=D_J mod 2=0. Every supplied chain homotopy equivalence
f:K->J is a chain isomorphism, with an inverse computable at every dyadic cut.

PROOF. Let g:J->K be its supplied homotopy inverse. The homotopy equalities
give gf=1 modulo 2 and fg=1 modulo 2, since both differentials vanish modulo
2. Thus in every degree the reduced matrices f_n and g_n are inverse matrices,
and the two free module ranks agree.

Set E_n=1-g_n f_n, whose entries lie in 2R. The series

  u_n = (sum_(j>=0) E_n^j) g_n                  (16)

satisfies u_n f_n=1. The analogous series using 1-f_n g_n constructs a right
inverse v_n. The equations u_n f_n=1 and f_n v_n=1 imply
u_n=u_n f_n v_n=v_n. Thus u_n is a two-sided inverse.

From f_(n-1)D_K=D_J f_n, multiplying by these inverses yields
D_K u_n=u_(n-1)D_J, so the inverse is a chain map. At precision m, the first
m terms of (16) suffice, and reduction of one residue gives the previous
residue. END.

When two reductions of the same source complex are already recorded, no new
comparison search is required. If their contractions are (i_K,p_K,h_K) and
(i_J,p_J,h_J), set

  f=p_J i_K,   g=p_K i_J.

Their explicit source homotopies give

  1-gf = D_K(p_K h_J i_K)+(p_K h_J i_K)D_K,

and the analogous formula for 1-fg. Formula (16) constructs the actual change
of minimal presentation. A different pivot or gauge choice therefore remains
connected by a returned chain isomorphism. There is no need to impose a
privileged basis or to average a witness over a noninvertible group order.

8. Faster compatible inverses by precision doubling
--------------------------------------------------
The finite geometric sums above establish existence and compatibility. To
compute more rapidly, let M=I+Q with Q in 2 End_R(V). Set U_0=I and

  U_(r+1)=U_r(2I-MU_r).                          (17)

Direct multiplication gives both residual identities

  I-MU_(r+1) = (I-MU_r)^2,
  I-U_(r+1)M = (I-U_rM)^2.                       (18)

Each residual therefore lies in 2^(2^r) End_R(V). After
ceil(log_2 m) updates the result is the inverse modulo 2^m. These are
noncommutative matrix identities: no commutativity of arbitrary matrices is
used. Using increasing precision retains exact compatibility because a
two-sided inverse in each quotient is unique.

Apply (17) to I+h delta in (8), or to g_nf_n in (16). This evaluates the
corrected reduction and the comparison of two minimal presentations with
O(log m) matrix multiplications per inverse, with the matrix dimensions and
the bit cost of arithmetic retained in the work account. It does not claim
O(log m) bit complexity or a polynomial bound in an unrelated original input
size. For an ideal m in a complete local ring, replace '2^(2^r)' by the ideal
power m^(2^r).

9. Coherence with base change, dagger, and finite cuts
----------------------------------------------------
Every formula is assembled from compositions, sums, supplied module maps,
and inverses characterized by equations. A filtration-preserving ring or
module representation carrying the data (1) and (7) carries the corrected
data (9). At a finite cut this is a literal finite algebraic statement.
Passing to compatible residues gives the continuous inverse-limit statement.

In particular a change of marked basis conjugates the corrected operators as
it conjugates the original operators. It does not require choosing a
gauge-fixed lift. This is consistent with the archived distinction between an
ordinary witness and a witness fixed by a group action.

For finite-free matrices with their dual pairing, taking transpose reverses
composition. The dual complex uses the reversed grading convention, i and p
exchange roles under transpose, and h becomes its transpose. The identity

  A^T = (1+delta^T h^T)^(-1)delta^T
      = delta^T(1+h^T delta^T)^(-1)

shows directly that the perturbation constructor commutes with this dual or
dagger presentation. This statement uses the algebraic dual pairing, not an
unstated positivity or inner-product norm on Z_2.

The same data may be represented by Hets/CASL module and matrix signatures,
by a differential graded category, or by interpreted proof-comparison
objects. The returned source witnesses come from (14)-(15). An institution
interpretation must carry that supplied proof data; satisfaction preservation
alone is not being used to invent a return on witnesses.

10. What this adds to the recovered Hilbert-24 work
--------------------------------------------------
The existing archive already contains exact slack calibration, finite cyclic
proof-grammar optimization, Pareto/support bookkeeping, schedule coherence,
locally inverse rule transport, syzygy obstructions, and compatible dyadic
solution constructors. The present construction gives additional operations:

  * Remove an explicitly contractible part of a comparison presentation while
    retaining a full reconstruction homotopy.
  * Certify the least possible number of generators in every comparison
    degree by one residue-complex calculation.
  * Maintain that attained minimality for an entire 2-adic deformation of the
    comparison rules, using explicit corrected differentials and return maps.
  * Compare any two minimal presentations by an actual chain isomorphism.
  * Return every comparison and its higher residual instead of discarding the
    discarded coordinates.
  * Compute each requested precision by a doubling recurrence, rather than
    redoing the reduction independently at each cut.

These are applications of known constructive homological tools to the
programme's retained comparison data. The source theorem in each domain still
determines the comparison complex and its interpretation. The universal
operations above then act on that data without re-proving the transport
principle afresh for each input.

11. Application to the retained native origami contraction
----------------------------------------------------------
The recovered Origami_LFunctions_Cocycles_Codimensions.md gives the cellular
boundary from three faces to six edges in the order (h1,h2,h3,v1,v2,v3):

       [ 1 -1  0 ]
       [-1  1  0 ]
  B =  [ 0  0  0 ],             boundary from edges to vertices = 0.
       [ 0  0  0 ]
       [ 0 -1  1 ]
       [ 0  1 -1 ]

Its recorded operators are

  R(a,b,c,d,e,f)=(a+b,c,d,e+f),
  S(A,B,C,D)=(A,0,B,C,D,0),
  K(a,b,c,d,e,f)=(-b,0,-f),
  w=(1,1,1)^T,     L(a,b,c)=b.

They satisfy RB=0, RS=I, SR+BK=I, Bw=0, Lw=1, wL+KB=I. They also satisfy
KS=0 and LK=0. Thus they are exactly the data (1), with h=K in degree one,
i_1=S, p_1=R, i_2=w, p_2=L, and retained differential zero. The source and
retained vertex modules are both R with identity return.

The ranks (1,4,1) in degrees (0,1,2) therefore attain the simultaneous lower
bound (5). This conclusion applies to every finite-free chain-homotopy
equivalent presentation of these marked cellular data, rather than only to
the exhibited basis. For the complete native 16-tile surface, the archived
component decomposition gives the analogous rank vector (12,28,12).

For an explicit formal change of the comparison boundary, let e_j denote edge
basis vectors and f_j face basis vectors, and put

  v=e_2+e_3,       delta=-2 v f_1^T.

This is an algebraic deformation of the chain data. A geometric realization
as a deformation of origami surfaces is not being asserted. Since the
edge-to-vertex differential is zero, (B+delta)^2=0 as a complex differential.
The correction can be evaluated symbolically:

  h delta = 2 f_1 f_1^T,
  (I+h delta)^(-1)=diag(1/3,1,1),
  A=-(2/3)(e_2+e_3)f_1^T,
  D'_2=-(2/3)(1,1,0,0)^T.

The complete changed return data are

  i'_2=(1/3,1,1)^T,          p'_2=L,
  i'_1=S,
  p'_1(a,b,c,d,e,f)=(a+b/3,c-2b/3,d,e+f),
  h'_1(a,b,c,d,e,f)=(-b/3,0,-f).

All coefficients are in Z_2 because 3 is a unit. The retained differential is
even, so the unchanged rank vector (1,4,1) is still globally minimal in the
specified presentation class. Its actual homology is now

  H_0 = Z_2,
  H_1 = Z_2^3 + Z_2/2Z_2,
  H_2 = 0.

This follows because (1,1,0,0) is primitive and -1/3 is a unit. The torsion
entry 2 remains visible in the minimal differential. The example illustrates
why the reduced differential and the source return must travel together:
minimum generator counts persist while the comparison obstruction changes.

Primary references
------------------
Marius Crainic, On the perturbation lemma, and deformations, 2004:
  https://arxiv.org/abs/math/0403266

Jun Chen, Yuming Liu, Guodong Zhou, Algebraic Morse theory via Homological
Perturbation Lemma, arXiv:2404.10165v2. In particular Section 1 records the
strong-deformation side conditions and perturbation identities, and the paper
develops constructive cancellation and minimality applications:
  https://arxiv.org/html/2404.10165v2

Lukas Vokrinek, Enriched categorical aspects of homological perturbation
theory, arXiv:2412.21182v1. Its differential graded and double-category
formulation addresses functoriality under composition and tensor products:
  https://arxiv.org/html/2412.21182v1

Historical novelty is not claimed for the homological perturbation lemma or
the minimal-complex theorems. The specific continuation here is their use as
portable proof-simplification and reconstruction constructors on the recovered
programme's comparison complexes.
10. A shortest original boundary witness and its independent dual certificate

Complete original source: Hilbert24_Constructive_Use.zip :: Hilbert24_Constructive_Use/MEDIAN_PROOF.txt

The fixed original integral origami boundary, source chain, homology class, representative and discrepancy are retained. Competitors are all integral face fillings of that same discrepancy, measured by signed face-occurrence L1 cost. The existing linear contraction is retained independently of the nonlinear optimizer. The general dual-certificate statement needs a supplied integer solution, positive rational weights, a dual covector satisfying every column bound, and equality for optimality. Coordinate changes transport the cost explicitly.

§2 Complete affine filling fibre; §3 Universal median theorem; §4 Dual certificate; §5 Uniform factor-two improvement; §7 Coordinate transport of cost and dual

HILBERT 24: A SHORTEST SOURCE BOUNDARY WITNESS
18 November 2026

1. Exact source and question

The recovered OrigamiCocycles.het defines the boundary of the three-square
native origami by

  D(u,v,w) = (u-v,v-u,0,0,w-v,v-w).

Its integral chain contraction, for x=(a,b,c,d,e,f), is

  R x = (a+b,c,d,e+f),
  S(A,B,C,F) = (A,0,B,C,F,0),
  K x = (-b,0,-f),
  omega = (1,1,1).

The source proves RD=0, RS=I, SR+DK=I and D omega=0. The source files
bundled here are byte copies, with exact hashes in source_manifest.json.

The question fixed here is: keep x, Rx and SRx; among ALL integral face
witnesses y satisfying x=SRx+D y, minimize

  |y_1|+|y_2|+|y_3|.

This is the cost of signed elementary face occurrences. It is a specific,
explicit simplicity criterion. It is not coefficient bit length or shared
proof-DAG storage. For example a coefficient N uses O(log N) bits even when
it denotes N signed face occurrences.

We retain the original linear map K. The optimal witness selector below is
nonlinear and is not substituted for K in the chain homotopy laws.

2. Construct the full competitor fibre, without enumerating fillings

The discrepancy is delta=x-SRx=(-b,b,0,0,-f,f). The equations D y=delta
are y_1-y_2=-b and y_3-y_2=-f. Therefore every witness has exactly the form

  y(t)=(t-b,t,t-f)=Kx+t omega,   t in Z.

Conversely each such vector has boundary delta by cancellation. This is an
explicit bijection Z -> {y : D y=delta}; its inverse reads the middle
coordinate. It constructs the complete affine family from the generator
omega of ker D.

3. The universal median theorem

Let l=min(0,b,f), h=max(0,b,f) and m=median(0,b,f). The median is computed
by the exact formula m=b+f-l-h, using comparisons and integer arithmetic.
Define

  y*=(m-b,m,m-f).

Then

  min_{D y=delta} ||y||_1 = h-l,
  ||y*||_1 = h-l.

Proof. Rewrite the objective as the sum of distances to the three marked
integers 0,b,f. After putting these three values in order l<=m<=h, it is

  |t-l|+|t-m|+|t-h|.

The triangle inequality gives |t-l|+|t-h|>=h-l. The middle summand is
nonnegative, so every t has cost at least h-l. At t=m the sum is
(m-l)+0+(h-m)=h-l. Equality forces |t-m|=0, hence t=m. Thus the optimal
face coefficient vector is unique in this declared fibre and cost.

The proof is constructive over integers with their decidable order. It
does not use enumeration, compactness, choice, a finite search bound,
classical selection of an optimizer, or a claim about global proof identity.
It applies to every six-coordinate integral chain, with no size cutoff.

4. A dual certificate that verifies optimality independently

Here is the general theorem used by verify_l1_certificate. Let A be an
integer matrix, delta an integer vector, x an actual integer solution of
A x=delta, w_j positive rational weights, and lambda a rational covector
such that |(A^T lambda)_j|<=w_j for every column. For every integer solution z,

  sum_j w_j |z_j| - lambda.delta
    = sum_j (w_j |z_j| - (A^T lambda)_j z_j) >= 0.

Each summand is nonnegative because the dual column bound controls both
signs. The equality is the pairing identity lambda.Az=(A^Tlambda).z.
Consequently a supplied x attaining equality is globally optimal over the
entire integer solution fibre. A nontight dual only certifies a lower bound;
the verifier returns optimality_certified=false in that case. No theorem
here asserts that a tight rational dual exists for every integer program.

For the native origami, put z=(b,0,f). If its minimum differs from its
maximum, select the first position i attaining l and the first position j
attaining h; set s_i=1, s_j=-1 and the remaining entry to zero. If all three
values agree, set s=0. Then sum s_i=0 and |s_i|<=1. Define, in the ORIGINAL
six-edge coordinates,

  lambda=(s_1,0,0,0,s_3,0).

Directly,

  D^T lambda=(s_1,-s_1-s_3,s_3)=s,
  lambda.delta=-s_1 b-s_3 f=h-l.

The primal cost of y* is also h-l. The returned integer lambda therefore
certifies minimum cost against every integer filling, not only against the
linear source witness Kx or the examples used in software validation.

5. A strict improvement on a whole family

For every N>=0, take x_N=(0,N,0,0,0,N). The original linear contraction
returns Kx_N=(-N,0,-N), of face-occurrence cost 2N. The median is N and
the optimized witness is (0,N,0), of cost N. Both return exactly the same
original discrepancy (-N,N,0,0,-N,N), homology class and representative.
For N>0 this is a strict factor-two improvement. For arbitrary b,f, the
saving is |b|+|f|-[max(0,b,f)-min(0,b,f)] and is always nonnegative.

The constructor has a fixed number of integer additions, comparisons and
sign changes. In a bit model its work scales with the input bit length.
Writing the fully expanded signed face sequence costs its output length.

6. What remains marked when proofs are compared

The optimization problem fixes the source chain, source homology class,
canonical representative and boundary. It permits the witness to vary by
a multiple of the fundamental 2-cycle omega. If a problem also fixes the
middle-coordinate normalization topRead(y)=0, the old witness Kx is the
unique member and there is no such improvement within that smaller fibre.
The new operation therefore sits alongside, rather than replaces, K.

In particular, the surface's cellular C3 is zero: the fundamental cycle
does not automatically provide a higher boundary between the two fillings.
The new object is a shorter witness of the same boundary assertion. Any
stronger proof-identity relation must declare what it identifies.

7. Transport of the result

For an invertible change of comparison coordinates x'=Vx and discrepancy
coordinates delta'=U delta, set A'=U A V^{-1}. Transport the cost by

  c'(x')=sum_j w_j |(V^{-1}x')_j|,

and the dual by lambda'=lambda U^{-1}. Then lambda'.delta'=lambda.delta
and c'(Vx)=c(x). The pairing proof transports, and actual source readback
returns the optimal witness. If V is a signed permutation preserving w,
the same coordinate formula sum w_j|x'_j| already equals the transported
cost. An arbitrary unimodular matrix need not preserve that coordinate norm.

Dyadic encoding can retain these exact integer coefficients and their cost
certificate. A finite 2-adic residue alone does not determine their real
absolute values. The intended route therefore keeps the integral source
and uses its exact decoder and cost, rather than optimizing an unmarked
residue after losing that information.

This joins the existing chain contraction, syzygy fibre, transpose/dagger
pairing and calibration machinery in an actual constructor that returns a
strictly smaller source proof on an infinite family.

8. Verification account

The .het companion records the same algebraic interfaces and theorem
targets with explicit premises. Hets/E are not available in this runtime;
no new Hets frontend or ATP success is claimed. The source identities,
universal proofs above, exact runtime certificates, and focused test report
are separate evidence. Historical source files retain their original hashes.
11. Original proof grade, residual energy, and a finite polynomial aperture

Complete original source: Hilbert_24_Collected_Proofs.zip :: Proofs/Hilbert24_Energy_Vacua_Return/hilbert24_energy_vacua_return/grade_inference/source/prior_continuation/energy_grade/Theorem_and_Proof.txt

An explicit finite acyclic grammar with retained ordered premise occurrences, finite signed integer prices and an inhabited selected root. The original HC interpretation must authenticate its rules, preserve judgment and work, and match the computed minimum with the fixed original calibration potential to obtain global HC minimality. The two-way HC test additionally needs zero-reflecting charge interpretation. The native Gram involution and six-stream residual inverse are the original supplied maps; the entire proof remains in the packet so tied minima stay distinct. The finite-width bound is in the given grammar size, with acquisition, authentication, potential binding and output costs retained.

§2 Retained grade–residual coupling; §3 All-minimum tight grammar; §4 Original HC binding (BIND) and zero reflection (FAITHFUL); §5 Finite-aperture theorem (5.1)–(5.2); §9 Constructed original proof normalizer

ORIGINAL PROOF GRADE, RESIDUAL ENERGY, AND A FINITE POLYNOMIAL APERTURE
23 November 2026

STATUS AND RESULT

This continuation constructs a new explicit coupling of three recovered
constructions. The recovered sources already provide (i) an actual Bellman
minimum producer for a finite acyclic proof grammar, (ii) the exact decoder
into the original HC proof calculus and its matching-potential minimum
theorem, and (iii) the original six-stream residual inverse and digit energy.
The new coupling retains the complete marked proof and encodes its Bellman
slack as that actual residual. Its zero-energy fibre is exactly the entire
minimum-proof fibre of the grammar. Under the archived source-rule and
original-potential bindings, this returns a minimum over EVERY admitted
original HC proof of the SAME judgement.

The new construction has a finite zero test. For an explicit grammar with
N vertices, maximum arity K and signed B-bit integer prices, the derived
width W=B+N*ceil(log2(K+1))+2 suffices for every valid grammar proof. Reading
only the first W residual digit planes then detects exact minimum grade.
No infinite-tail test or enumeration of proof trees is needed.

The new Hets bridge is supplied in formal/Proof_Grade_Residual_Bridge.het.
Hets 0.108.0 static analysis passed on the full composed entry point with
its exact recovered dependency closure; see formal/formal_validation.json.
This checks syntax, signatures and dependencies, not automated theorem
discharge. The mathematical proofs below remain separately supplied.

1. THE RECOVERED MAPS, WITH THEIR ORIGINAL TYPES

1.1 Finite grammar and exact Bellman producer.

An actual input P has N indexed vertices and explicit rules
  r=(head(r); children(r); c_r),
where the ordered child list retains EVERY premise occurrence and each child
index is strictly smaller than its head. Rule identities and source marks
are retained. Prices c_r are finite signed integers. Rational prices first
use their explicitly constructed common positive denominator.

A proof p is an original tree of these rule applications. Its grade is
  C(node(r,p_1,...,p_k))=c_r+sum_i C(p_i).
The recovered tdpEvaluate computes, in topological order,
  d(v)=min_(r:head(r)=v) [c_r+sum_i d(child_i)],
with infinity meaning no finite proof. It retains an original attaining rule.

The full archived proof proves both sides:
  d(v)<=C(p) for EVERY valid original P-proof p of v;
  if d(v) is finite, tdpUnfold(d,v,N) constructs a proof p_* with C(p_*)=d(v).
The independent certificate checks local Bellman inequalities, the actual
selected source rule, ordered premises, attaining value and topology.

Sources: aperture_mtheory/tropical_proofs.het, TDPFiniteReturns;
tropical_proofs.txt sections 3--7. These files are copied exactly in sources/.

1.2 Exact return to the original HC calculus.

The original decoder is literal source code:
  tdpOriginalProof(P,tdpStep(r,f))=
    hcStep(tdpJudgement(P,tdpHead(r)),tdpCharge(tdpPrice(r)),
           tdpOriginalForest(P,f)).

The recovered structural induction proves
  conclusion(Decode_P(p))=tdpJudgement(P,root(p)),
  Work(Decode_P(p))=tdpCharge(C(p)).
TDPSourceRuleBinding(P) authenticates each listed rule as an actual admitted
HC rule at those same premises, conclusion and charge. It then gives
hcValid(Decode_P(p)). Numeric optimization alone does not establish this
source authentication.

The original calibration is a fixed potential V on original judgements.
For every admitted rule, c+sum V(premises)-V(conclusion) is nonnegative.
Whole-tree induction gives
  Excess(q)=Work(q)-V(conclusion(q))>=0
for every valid original HC proof q. A valid zero-excess q therefore has
minimum work among ALL original admitted proofs of its judgement.

The archived TDPOriginalProofReturns already states that if
  tdpCharge(C(p))=V(tdpJudgement(P,root(p))),
then Decode_P(p) has hcExcess=zero, hcMinimum and hcNativeMinimum. This is
the exact existing grade-to-original-minimum return used below. We do not
replace this V by a new potential without declaring a different calculus.

Sources: completion_paths/proof_calibration.het, HCWholeProofReturns and
HCMinimumReturn; aperture_mtheory/tropical_proofs.het, TDPOriginalProofData
and TDPOriginalProofReturns.

1.3 The recovered residual inverse.

On the actual six-stream native coefficient space X=Z_2^6, the native Gram
dagger D satisfies D^2=I. Set
  H0=I-2D,             H0^-1=-(I+2D)/3,
  R_b(x)=H0*x-b,       Restore(b,r)=H0^-1*(r+b).
The odd integer 3 is a unit in Z_2. Multiplying I-2D by I+2D gives -3I,
so these are actual two-sided inverse formulas, not assumed solutions.

The original completed digit energy is
  E_b(x)=(1/6) sum_(i=1..6) sum_(j>=0) 2^(-j-1) bit_j(R_b(x)_i).
Its nonnegative summands have total weight one. The recovered proof gives
  E_b(x)=0 iff R_b(x)=0 iff x=H0^-1*b.
It also gives exact finite energy cuts and their geometric tail estimate.

Source: hilbert24_paths/residual_energy.het and residual_energy.txt.

1.4 The already recovered real-valued proof-energy map is distinct.

HCFChargeReturns supplies an explicitly selected additive ORDER-REFLECTING
valuation hcfScore:Charge->R. It proves
  hcfScore(Work(q))=hcfScore(V(phi))+hcfScore(Excess(q)),
and exact equivalence of original charge comparison and real comparison.
This is already a faithful energy representation of original proof grade.
The native residual digit energy below has a different role: it is an exact
ZERO DETECTOR for the retained integer slack, not an order embedding.

Source: hilbert24_paths/central_return.het and central_return.txt sections 1--5.

2. NEW THEOREM: THE RETAINED GRADE-RESIDUAL COUPLING

Fix P and a vertex v with d(v) finite. For every valid P-proof p of v set
  Delta(p)=C(p)-d(v) in N,
  iota(Delta)=(Delta,0,0,0,0,0) in Z_2^6,
  sigma_b(p)=H0^-1*(b+iota(Delta(p))),
  Packet_b(p)=(P,b,p,sigma_b(p)).

The theorem is
  E_b(sigma_b(p))=0
    iff Delta(p)=0
    iff C(p)=d(v)
    iff p is a minimum-price proof of v in the ORIGINAL grammar P.

PROOF.
Bellman's lower-bound theorem gives Delta(p)>=0. By the actual inverse law,
  R_b(sigma_b(p))=iota(Delta(p)).
The completed energy is zero iff all digits of this vector vanish. The
ordinary integer inclusion into Z_2 is zero-reflecting: a positive integer
has a finite binary expansion containing a 1, and its first nonzero bit
remains nonzero in every sufficiently deep quotient Z/2^m. Therefore the
residual is zero iff Delta(p)=0.

If Delta=0, C(p)=d(v), and the Bellman lower bound proves minimality against
every competitor. Conversely, the recovered finite unfolding produces p_*
with C(p_*)=d(v). Any minimum p must have C(p)<=C(p_*)=d(v); the lower bound
gives the opposite inequality, hence equality. This proves every direction.

ATTAINMENT IS CONSTRUCTED.
Compute d and the archived source backpointers, unfold them denotationally
to p_*, and apply Packet_b. Its energy is zero. The compact producer stores
the source proof by backpointers; fully printing an exponential tree is not
claimed to take polynomial time.

ALL TIES ARE RETAINED.
Distinct minimum proofs p and q have the same coordinate H0^-1*b, but
Packet_b(p)=Packet_b(q) iff p=q, since the whole marked proof is retained.
The zero-energy packet fibre is therefore canonically equivalent to the
whole minimum-proof fibre. A bare six-stream coordinate would erase ties;
the actual packet does not.

3. NEW THEOREM: ONE COMPACT GRAMMAR RETAINS ALL MINIMUM PROOFS

Call an original rule tight when its head and children are finite and
  c_r+sum_i d(child_i)=d(head(r)).
Construct P_min by scanning the original rule list and retaining EVERY tight
rule, with its original mark and ordered child occurrences. Keep the same
vertex set and topological order. This is a linear number of graph visits
after the table is constructed, with the actual integer work paid.

For every original tree p,
  Valid_(P_min)(p) iff p is a minimum P-proof of its root.

PROOF.
At a valid proof node define
  epsilon(r)=c_r+sum_i d(child_i)-d(head(r))>=0.
The child contributions telescope exactly:
  Delta(node(r,p_1,...,p_k))=epsilon(r)+sum_i Delta(p_i).
Every summand is nonnegative, including every repeated child occurrence.
The sum is zero iff the root rule is tight and all child slacks are zero.
Structural induction proves exactly that the full proof uses only tight
rules. The converse is the same induction. No tie is chosen or discarded.

An original deterministic first-tight backpointer still gives one witness.
P_min describes all witnesses simultaneously, without enumerating them.
Different marks on equal-price rules remain different marked alternatives.

4. NEW THEOREM: EXACT BINDING TO THE ORIGINAL HC POTENTIAL

Let phi=tdpJudgement(P,v). Retain TDPSourceRuleBinding(P), and establish the
specific existing-potential identity
  tdpCharge(d(v))=V(phi).                                  (BIND)
The candidate names this equality PGBoundMatches(P,v).

Then for every valid P-proof p of v,
  hcExcess(Decode_P(p))=tdpCharge(Delta(p)).                 (4.1)

PROOF.
The original decoder preserves Work=tdpCharge(C(p)), and its conclusion is
phi. Original HC calibration therefore gives
  Excess=tdpCharge(C(p))-V(phi)
        =tdpCharge(C(p))-tdpCharge(d(v))
        =tdpCharge(C(p)-d(v)).
The final equality follows from the archived additive charge map: additive
maps of groups preserve negation by cancellation. This proves (4.1).

Consequently zero residual energy gives Delta=0, then original excess zero,
then hcMinimum and hcNativeMinimum. These last predicates quantify over the
FULL original HC proof universe of phi, not just the finite grammar image.
The original judgement, whole proof and every charged premise occurrence
return unchanged under the decoder and native proof chart.

For a two-way equivalence with the original HC minimum test, also require
that the declared integer charge interpretation reflects zero:
  tdpCharge(z)=zero iff z=0.                               (FAITHFUL)
This holds for literal integer grade, and for its positive rational/real
unit scaling. The recovered tdpCharge axioms by themselves state additivity
only; silently assuming faithfulness would add an unjustified inference.

Under BIND and FAITHFUL,
  residual energy zero iff original HC excess zero
    iff Decode_P(p) is an original HC minimum
    iff its native encoding is an original native minimum.

For the reverse HC-minimum direction, use the already constructed p_* as
an original valid competitor. Its HC work is V(phi). An original HC minimum
has work <=V(phi), while original calibration gives >=V(phi). Antisymmetry
gives equality, and FAITHFUL returns Delta=0. No completeness of P as a
presentation of every original proof is required for this argument.

The set of ALL original HC minimizers need not lie in this particular P.
The theorem retains every tie in the stated source image and proves its
minimum value against the whole HC class. It does not invent a grammar
covering all outside proofs.

5. NEW FINITE-APERTURE THEOREM AND EXPLICIT WIDTH

Let each signed input price have at most B bits, N be the vertex count,
K the maximum rule arity, and put
  W=B+N*ceil(log2(K+1))+2.
Every valid proof has height at most N and at most
  1+K+...+K^(N-1) <= (K+1)^N
node occurrences. Using |c_r|<=2^(B-1),
  |C(p)| <= 2^(B-1)*(K+1)^N <= 2^(W-3).
The finite optimum d(v) is attained by such a proof, so it obeys the same
absolute-value bound. Therefore
  0<=Delta(p)<=2^(W-2)<2^W                               (5.1)
for EVERY valid grammar proof, not merely the selected one. The conservative
width also covers standard endpoint conventions for signed bit lengths.

Hence all residual digits at positions j>=W are zero, and
  E_b(sigma_b(p))=E_(b,W)(sigma_b(p))
    = reverse_W(Delta(p)) / (6*2^W).                     (5.2)
Here reverse_W reverses the W binary digits, including leading zero padding.
It is a wiring permutation, not an arithmetic approximation.

Therefore the W-plane zero test reflects EXACT Delta=0 and all the minimum
conclusions above. No real-number comparison or limiting equality test is
required. If Delta>0, the exact positive gap satisfies
  E_b(sigma_b(p)) >= 1/(6*2^W).
This quantitative separation is for the declared finite grammar width;
there is no single positive gap uniform over unbounded grammar widths.

FINITE-DIGIT PROOF.
For n<2^m write n=2q+e with e in {0,1}. The first digit is e and the next
m-1 digits are those of q<2^(m-1). Induction on m gives: the first m digits
all vanish iff n=0, and all later digits vanish. The Hets candidate defines
pgHalf, pgLowBit and pgDigit recursively and explicitly binds hreBit(pgNat(n),j)
to those actual digits in PGStandardDigits. It does not infer standard
finite-bit behavior from arbitrary separated digit labels.

6. THE POLYNOMIAL BILL AND THE FREDKIN CONNECTION

The recovered indexed Bellman evaluator uses
  O((N+M+S)*(W+log(N+M+S+1))) bit operations,
where M is the number of rules and S counts all premise occurrences.
The all-tight mask is one more scan with exact W-bit scores/comparisons.
For a supplied compact proof DAG, compute its unfolded grade by a DAG fold
that adds every child reference with its original multiplicity. This is
polynomial in the compact DAG size and W; it does not require tree expansion.

The new adapter adds a W-bit subtraction C(p)-d(v), a bit-reversal wiring
and a W-bit zero test. Its Boolean circuit size is O(W), beyond the grade
evaluation already paid. To output m native coordinate bits, evaluate the
fixed H0^-1 on b+iota(Delta): O(m) elementary bit work with fixed odd-division
transducers, plus the original acquisition of b's m-bit prefix. The zero
test can use m=W. The full bill retains
  Acquire(P) + authenticate original rules + bind original potential
  + Bellman/all-ties work + compact proof-grade fold
  + O(W+m) + Acquire_b(m) + actual output/checking.
These acquisition/authentication terms are not deleted by the chart.

The recovered tropical_proofs.txt section 11 already compiles a Boolean
circuit with g gates and o requested bits to a clean retained Fredkin
computation with at most 4g+o Fredkin gates using its stated gate gadgets.
The source is kept; requested dual-rail outputs survive; all computation
workspace is restored by reversing the actual gate word. The companion
Fredkin implementation in this continuation can compile the new O(W)
subtraction and zero-detector along with the Bellman/tight-rule evaluator.
Exact primitive constants must follow that implementation's chosen gadgets.
Semantic inverse cancellation never refunds executed gates.

7. TWO NECESSARY DISTINCTIONS, WITH CONCRETE COUNTEREXAMPLES

7.1 Digit energy is not ordered integer grade.
For one nonzero residual coordinate,
  Energy(iota(1))=1/12,
  Energy(iota(2))=1/24.
Thus 1<2 but its digit energy is larger. Our theorem uses the constructed
attained baseline d(v), nonnegative slack and exact zero reflection. It
does not use monotonicity of positive residual energies. HCF's real charge
valuation is the archived order-reflecting representation when one is needed.

7.2 An ambient nonzero-cylinder minimum need not be a source proof.
Take a one-vertex grammar with two nullary rules of prices 0 and 3. Its
slacks are exactly {0,3}. At depth one, the marked residual prefix with
first coordinate 1 and all other coordinates 0 has a nonempty proof image:
the price-3 rule lies in it. SHEEnergyReturns constructs the ambient
cylinder minimizer with residual (1,0,0,0,0,0), but no source proof has
slack 1. The ambient minimizer is outside this particular proof image.
The image point has energy 1/8, while the ambient lower bound is 1/12.

In general the ambient minimizer for prefix p lies in the retained proof
image precisely when value(p)=iota(Delta(q)) for some valid source proof q.
That membership must be constructed. The zero-prefix ground used by the
new theorem is guaranteed to be in the image by the Bellman attainer p_*.
This is why the exact source minimum follows here without making a false
claim about arbitrary nonzero constrained cylinders.

8. PORTABILITY AND EVIDENCE

The complete mathematical proof uses finite structural/topological
induction, ordered integer arithmetic, the explicit original ring inverse,
the binary expansion of a natural number, and the archived HC calibration.
It uses no choice of an arbitrary witness family: first-tight construction
is deterministic and all ties are retained by a finite rule filter.

For another institution, transport the actual source rule interpretation,
proof decoder and their exact grade/judgement equations. The recovered
HCFProofChart and HCNativeMinimumReturns already supply exact proof/charge
return. An institution satisfaction map alone does not supply proof terms.

Sources are preserved byte for byte in sources/ with SHA-256 and original
locations in source_provenance.json. source_excerpts.txt contains the exact
recovered declarations most relevant to the coupling. The new candidate
does not change those source declarations or the original HC potential.

9. COMPLETED ADJOINING RETURN: CONSTRUCTED MINIMUM TO PROOF NORMALIZATION

The targeted inference_lifts source contains a further actual constructor,
SLISuppliedMinimumReturns. Given a valid zero-excess original proof p_*,
sliConstantMove(p_*) returns that literal proof on every proof input of the
same judgement. sliConstantPaths(p_*) packages this as an admitted HPO path;
sliSingletonPool keeps the actual target proof and a point prior. The source
proves the entire SLIProofNormalization contract, not just a scalar saving.

Our Bellman constructor now supplies p_* through the exact original decoder
under BIND. Substitution completes this adjacent route:
  actual grammar -> Bellman attainer -> zero residual packet
    -> original zero-excess HC proof -> actual original proof normalizer.

For an admitted original source pool Q and law q, the recovered identity is
  FreeGain = mean_q(original excess) + KL(q || original prior)/beta.
The actual same-goal proof cost and the actual prior are retained. The
normalizer may select the deterministic first-tight proof for execution,
while P_min still retains all tied proofs as a separate complete fibre.

Proof_Grade_Inference_Return.het records this new short composition using
only PGFiniteApertureReturns and the existing SLISuppliedMinimumReturns.
Its proof is direct substitution of the validity, original conclusion and
zero-excess fields already constructed above. The normalizer's actual
