%% bits; neither can turn a scalar estimate into the missing lower proof.
%%
%% FULL exact complexity equivalence (not just the graph or parity case):
%% E_C computable in deterministic polynomial bit-time for every C
%% iff P=NP. Forward, test E_C=0 and apply Cook-Levin. Reverse, assuming
%% P=NP, the uniform language [(C,k): exists a, A_C(a)<=k] is in P since
%% an original assignment verifies the inequality in polynomial time.
%% Binary search 0..number of clauses gives E_C; recover a minimizing
%% assignment by asking the same threshold question under successive
%% pins, testing the cost of the ORIGINAL clauses (pins are constraints,
%% not extra penalties). This also gives a uniform algorithm for the
%% two outputs (exact value and attaining assignment). This reverse
%% direction does not assert a polynomial-size proof of the optimum in
%% every separately chosen formal proof system.
%% The explicit bucket algorithm pays for its generated factor tables,
%% which can have exponential arity cost. The iff theorem does not
%% certify a uniform polynomial budget for that algorithm.
%%
%% Exact finite Euclidean path integral comparison:
%% q is number of clauses and M=2^n is number of assignments.
%% exp(-beta E) <= Z_C(exp(-beta)) <= M exp(-beta E).
%% Hence 0 <= E + beta^{-1} log Z_C(exp(-beta)) <= n log(2)/beta.
%% Take beta=8(n+1) log(2): free energy is within strictly 1/8 of E.
%% A numerical approximation with error at most 1/8 permits nearest
%% integer recovery. This is equivalent to P=NP as well: under that
%% hypothesis compute E by threshold and output E as the approximation.
%% Positive Gibbs weights concentrate on minimizers; tied minima
%% remain multiple paths. This is not quantum measurement. In the
%% real-time Feynman integral amplitudes carry oscillatory phases.
%% https://journals.aps.org/rmp/abstract/10.1103/RevModPhys.20.367
%%
%% CASL local interface below mirrors the proved two-way order law.
%% The abstract linear-order laws are imported as premises, not as
%% their own conclusions; the min selector is given by the two cases.

spec BCHilbert24TropicalData =
  sorts Grade
  ops zeroGrade : Grade;
      tmin : Grade * Grade -> Grade
  pred below : Grade * Grade
  forall a,b,c : Grade
  . below(a,a)
  . below(a,b) /\ below(b,c) => below(a,c)
  . below(a,b) \/ below(b,a)
  . below(a,b) /\ below(b,a) => a = b
  . below(zeroGrade,a)
  . below(a,b) => tmin(a,b) = a
  . below(b,a) => tmin(a,b) = b
end

spec BCHilbert24TropicalReturns =
  BCHilbert24TropicalData then
  forall a,b,z : Grade
  . below(tmin(a,b),a) /\ below(tmin(a,b),b)
      %(h24_ground_value_is_lower_bound)% %implied
  . tmin(a,b) = a \/ tmin(a,b) = b
      %(h24_ground_path_attains)% %implied
  . below(z,tmin(a,b)) <=> (below(z,a) /\ below(z,b))
      %(h24_ground_value_pastes_negative_evidence)% %implied
  . tmin(a,b) = zeroGrade <=> (a = zeroGrade \/ b = zeroGrade)
      %(h24_zero_selects_original_branch)% %implied
end

%% Haar, Maxwell-Fresnel and Stokes readout of the SAME Hilbert 24 action
%% -------------------------------------------------------------------
%% On Z_2 with normalized Haar measure, inverse parity branch B_w of
%% length k maps onto the marked cylinder C_w and pushes Haar to its
%% conditional probability. The branch slope is 2^k/3^s. The odd factor
%% preserves Haar and multiplication by 2^k scales unnormalized measure
%% by 2^-k. Thus mu(C_w)=2^-k and each next-bit child has conditional
%% mass 1/2. The parity coordinate reads the original assignment a(x).
%%
%% Use normalized Jones amplitude u(x)=(1,chi(x))/sqrt(2), where chi=+1
%% on the child marked 0 and -1 on the child marked 1. It encodes the
%% question, not a choice of which child has the better answer.
%% A normal-incidence lossless interface has wave impedance
%% eta_j=sqrt(mu_j/epsilon_j); use eta_j>0. Maxwell tangential E and H
%% matching gives 1+r=t, (1-r)/eta_1=t/eta_2, so
%% r=(eta_2-eta_1)/(eta_2+eta_1), R=|r|^2. In flux units its reflected
%% and transmitted outputs have powers summing to the incident power.
%% Choosing eta_2=3 eta_1 gives r=1/2, R=1/4. This comes from E AND H
%% matching even in nonmagnetic media; no static magnet is required.
%%
%% In an ideal routed cascade pick the reflected port h times for each
%% failed original clause, bypass for every satisfied clause. Independent
%% routed stages are essential: a bare multilayer has interfering returns.
%% The Jones amplitude in the selected port is v=2^(-h A_C(a)) u.
%% Let J_w = integral on C_w of vv*, conditional on C_w. Its trace
%% P_w^(h) is the conditional average of 4^(-h A_C(a)).
%% At the root this is 2^-n Z_C(4^-h). J_w=(J_w0+J_w1)/2 when all three
%% matrices use the same analyzer calibrated to the parent question.
%% The H/V, D/A and circular pairs are six analyzer intensities, not
%% six independent Stokes coordinates. Define S0=tr J, S2=2 Re J_12.
%% On the parent, D/A are the +/- eigenpolarizations of the child flag:
%% S0=(P_w0+P_w1)/2 and S2=(P_w0-P_w1)/2. Therefore S0 +/- S2 return
%% the two ORIGINAL conditional energies. Keeping the analyzer mark
%% is required for this inverse.
%%
%% Effective finite spectral separation: with d unread original bits,
%% 2^d P_w^(h) = N_0(w) + sum_{A>0} 4^(-h A).
%% If 2h>=d+2 the tail is at most 1/4; round exactly to obtain N_0(w).
%% Precision is an independent issue: for this normalized averaging,
%% changing a single assignment from unit action to zero action changes
%% the root power by 2^-n (1-4^-h). An aggregate scalar reading at
%% fixed absolute error therefore does not implement this count in
%% general. Local polarized child return retains a useful question but
%% does not by itself supply an input-polynomial acquisition procedure.
%%
%% Fresnel normal incidence: https://www.ee.iitb.ac.in/course/~vel/apps/NormalIncidanceinMediumInterfaceAirDielectric/
%% Coherency transform: https://www.wip.osapublishing.org/josa/abstract.cfm?uri=josa-55-8-969

%% Source identification and observable comorphism, not an analogy.
%% The archived Source reading notes, message 33966f97-d6f1-5adc-b1dc-
%% 71fc11d8fe05, section 2, gives F(u,v)=[[u-v,2v],[2u,v-u]],
%% F^2=(u+v)^2 Id, F* diag(u,v) F=(u+v)^2 diag(u,v), and the
%% four-lane block F(u,v) direct-sum F(x,y). On one incident port take
%% u:v=3:1, with u,v admittances. F(3,1)/4 has first diagonal entry 1/2.
%% Both polarizations may use that same block at normal incidence.
%% This specialization makes 1/4 a power ratio, not a dyadic unit:
%% over Z_2 keep the denominator-cleared equation; to normalize use Q_2
%% with its precision shift (or use R for optical positivity).
%% The archived chart is L=1-2D, N=-(1+2D)/3, with D^2=Id and LN=NL=Id.
%% This N is dyadically integral; it is not division by the Fresnel sum.
%%
%% Explicit observable comorphism (real power fragment):
%% signatures have finite labelled parent apertures with two marked
%% children each. Maps reindex parents and may swap a child pair.
%% Source models have nonnegative pairs (P_0,P_1). Target models are
%% calibrated observable pairs (S_0,S_2) with S_0 >= abs(S_2), retaining
%% the same parent and child-address marks. Use discrete model categories.
%% Phi retains the signature with its labels. Alpha substitutes
%% P_0 -> S_0+S_2 and P_1 -> S_0-S_2 in each affine equality or inequality
%% (and applies the same substitution recursively through connectives).
%% Beta is the model reduct given by that same numeric pair. Evaluation
%% before and after substitution is identical; induction on sentences
%% proves satisfaction. Under a swap, S_2 changes sign while S_0 does
%% not, so both naturality squares commute. A model section is
%% (P_0,P_1) -> ((P_0+P_1)/2,(P_0-P_1)/2); both inverses follow by
%% addition/subtraction. On the admitted image, proof predicates
%% and their original assignments or clause witnesses can be retained
%% as a separate dependent field and returned by the same equality.
%% This does not derive an original certificate from Stokes data alone.
%%
%% Earlier full-packet chart and denominator-cleared source:
%% https://chatgpt.com/c/6b055ad6-cee0-83ea-874e-305e415cca75?messageId=33966f97-d6f1-5adc-b1dc-71fc11d8fe05

%% Native Gram elimination on the full original CNF
%% ------------------------------------------------
%% Use the archived Z_2[S3] -> Z_2[C2] x M2(Z_2) faithful 4x4 chart,
%% with J=[[1,0,0,1],[0,2,-1,0],[0,-1,2,0],[1,0,0,2]] and
%% U^dagger=J^(-1) U^T J. Write R,S for the actual frame matrices of
%% rotation and reflection. R^dagger=R^(-1), S^dagger=S. The frame Gram
%% Lambda=(I-R)^dagger(I-R)+(I-S)^dagger(I-S)/2
%%       =3I-R-R^(-1)-S, using the integral CANCELLED expression.
%% On (e1,e4), Lambda=[[2,0],[-1,0]]. On (e2,e3), Lambda=4I-S_std,
%% since R_std+R_std^(-1)=-I, with S_std^2=I. The latter block has the
%% two-sided inverse (4I+S_std)/15. The entry -1 and determinant 15
%% are units at every aperture: no averaging over six or dividing by
%% 2 in the native integral computation. Thus ker Lambda=span(e4)
%% over each Z/2^m as well as Z_2. Reading e4 is a constructive
%% inverse to inserting that fixed Gram lane.
%%
%% Write D_j for the diagonal original clause-failure projector in
%% the ASSIGNMENT basis; each entry is 0 or 1. Stack the constraints
%%   (I tensor Lambda) z = 0,  (D_j tensor I) z = 0 for every j.
%% At any nonzero dyadic aperture, the first forces z=v tensor e4.
%% A coordinate a survives the latter equations iff all D_j(a)=0,
%% because any failing j imposes the unit equation v_a=0.
%% Conversely v supported on models extends by e4. This is an
%% explicit inverse on solution modules, not a spectral analogy.
%% In particular the stacked test is exact already over F_2.
%% Do NOT replace the stack by the sum over F_2: two failures cancel.
%%
%% Over R positivity DOES allow the Gram sum. In the frame metric J,
%% the self-adjoint operator T=sum_j D_j tensor I + I tensor Lambda
%% has eigenvalues A_C(a)+lambda with lambda in {0,2,3,5}. Thus its
%% lowest eigenvalue is the exact tropical action E_C. For m>=1 clauses,
%% B=I-T/(m+5) is PSD with norm 1-E_C/(m+5), in the same Gram metric.
%% An additive norm error <1/(2(m+5)) suffices to decide SAT. This
%% removes the exponentially small intensity scale of Haar averaging.
%% It prices PRECISION and gives a uniform POLYNOMIAL description:
%% use the original clause projectors and the constant frame block.
%% It is not a proof of polynomial time for evaluating that norm on
%% a succinct operator of dimension 4*2^n. Nor is it a lower bound
%% against every possible algorithm. Native normalization on a
%% self-adjoint block sends it to its negative; the specified
%% transported product and readback undo that coordinate change.

%% Arbitrary succinct CNF: small exact Gram, and the moment route's limit
%% ---------------------------------------------------------------------
%% After frame elimination use A(a)=number of failed original clauses.
%% Let D=diag(A), u=uniform unit vector, and q=number of clauses.
%% The cyclic space span{u,D u,...,D^q u} has dimension at most q+1,
%% is D-invariant (D satisfies product_{j=0}^q(D-j I)=0), and sees
%% EVERY present action: the projector to level j sends u to the
%% nonzero vector supported on that level whenever it is inhabited.
%% Thus it preserves the least action of the entire original operator,
%% including zero. It does not form a basis by enumerating assignments.
%%
%% Exact compressed pencil: write M_i = E_Haar[A^i]. Define
%% G_ij=M_(i+j) and K_ij=M_(i+j+1) for 0<=i,j<=q. For coefficients c,
%% c^T G c = norm(p(D)u)^2 and c^T K c is its energy.
%% Nullspace(G) exactly identifies zero test vectors and is also
%% killed by K, so passing to the quotient is exact. Its Rayleigh
%% minimum is E_C. Raw moments M_0 through M_q already suffice for
%% the zero test alone: P_0(t)=product_{j=1}^q(1-t/j) is exactly the
%% indicator at 0 on {0,...,q}; E_Haar[P_0(A)] is model mass.
%% Distinct from the Gram-norm approximation: the moment route uses
%% exact inputs. This is valid for every finite CNF including q=0.
%%
%% Compute marked factorial moments directly from original clauses:
%% H_l = E_Haar[binomial(A,l)] = sum_{|J|=l} mu(intersection_{j in J} U_j).
%% Each non-tautological U_j pins its original falsifying pattern.
%% The contribution for J is 0 if two patterns disagree on a common
%% variable; otherwise it is 2^(-number of distinct pinned variables).
%% This is a clause-overlap acquisition formula, not assignment scans.
%% H_0=1 and E_Haar[P_0(A)]=sum_l (-1)^l H_l by (1-1)^A.
%% Raw moments follow from the expansion of powers in falling
%% factorials. For fixed l the above formula is polynomial; the
%% general formula still has exponentially many subsets in q.
%% A polynomial algorithm for all exact H_l is stronger than the
%% decision target: it would also compute exact model counts.
%%
%% Exact obstruction to replacing high-order marked information by
%% any fixed order of unmarked moments: take a cycle on r>=3 vertices.
%% Variables are edge bits; each vertex XOR equation is encoded by its
%% two forbidden local tuples. Each violated vertex fails exactly ONE
%% original clause. The incidence map has image the even-parity
%% subspace (leaf elimination on a spanning tree) and each image vector
%% has exactly two preimages. Thus for even vs odd original charge
%% the failure histograms are (1+z)^r+(1-z)^r (SAT) and
%% (1+z)^r-(1-z)^r (UNSAT), respectively. Every derivative at z=1
%% of order <r, and hence every raw or factorial Haar moment of order
%% <r, agrees. The retained total-charge Walsh mode nevertheless
%% distinguishes them with one original XOR calculation. The Gram
%% compression is exact; trying to realize it using only low-order
%% unmarked moments is not. This example is no general time lower bound.

%% Two-bit Bayes and the original H24 Fano obstruction.
%% Set Z_C(z)=sum_a z^A(a), where A counts failed original clauses.
%% At q=4^(-h), 2h>=n+2, the single scalar R=Z_C(q/4)/Z_C(q)
%% is <=1/4 on UNSAT and >=4/5 on SAT. Each term in the ratio is the
%% posterior mean of 4^-A. If there is a model, total nonsolution
%% weight is <=2^n q<=1/4, while model weight is >=1. Thus an additive
%% quarter read returns the original SAT bit by strict threshold 1/2.
%% It determines the original model by restriction, or the Cech section
%% by scanning for a false original clause on the challenged assignment.
%%
%% For the Fano core F from Hilbert24_Marked_Aperture (the memoizer's
%% 7^k counterfamily), pair every positive triple clause with its negative.
%% Each monochromatic block is exactly one original failed clause.
%% Each bichromatic block contains exactly two crossing pairs.
%% More generally in a triple system with every pair in exactly lambda
%% blocks, A = b - lambda*a*(v-a)/2, where a is the number of black
%% variables, v the number of variables, and b the number of blocks.
%% Thus Z_F(z)=sum_{a=0}^v binom(v,a) z^(b-lambda*a*(v-a)/2).
%% Paired clause recognition and the pair-incidence test are polynomial.
%% For lambda>0 and v>=7 this gives strictly positive minimum energy.
%% After any pins, let a0 be the pinned black count and u the unread count.
%% Replace a by a0+j and binom(v,a) by binom(u,j), j=0..u. This produces
%% the exact four child masses at every two-bit cut in polynomial bit time.
%% In particular H(z)=70z+42z^2+14z^4+2z^7 for the Fano core;
%% disjoint (x OR y OR w) padding multiplies H(z) by (7+z)^k.
%% No padding assignments are required to check the negative read.
%% On challenge to the original Cech section, find a monochromatic block
%% in the actual original assignment and return its positive clause if
%% all bits are 0, negative clause if all are 1. Insert it as first vertex
%% in the Cech contraction. This uses the original clause mark, even
%% though the histogram used only the count a. Covers the infinite base.
%%
%% More general quotient for paired triples: replace the constant pair
%% incidence by a marked pair-weight matrix w_ij (number of triple
%% occurrences containing {i,j}). Then A=b-cut_w/2. If the coordinates
%% have t classes with w_ij constant on each pair of classes (off-diagonal
%% within each class), the energy depends only on the black counts a_i.
%% The cut contributions are w_ii*a_i*(v_i-a_i) within each class and
%% w_ij*(a_i*(v_j-a_j)+a_j*(v_i-a_i)) across i<j. The multiplicity is
%% the product of binomial coefficients (adjusted for pins). This folds
%% in polynomial bit time for fixed t, including dense primal graphs.
%% The scalar computation does not replace the original clause return.
%% No all-CNF polynomial quotient bound is proved by this construction.

spec PairedTripleCutLocal = 
  free type Color ::= white | black
  sort Nat
  ops cross : Color * Color -> Nat;
      cuts : Color * Color * Color -> Nat;
      monochrome : Color * Color * Color -> Nat
  ops zero, one, two : Nat; plus : Nat * Nat -> Nat
  vars a,b,c : Color
  . cross(white,white)=zero
  . cross(black,black)=zero
  . cross(white,black)=one
  . cross(black,white)=one
  . cuts(a,b,c)=plus(plus(cross(a,b),cross(b,c)),cross(a,c))
  . monochrome(a,b,c)=one <=> (a=b /\ b=c)
  . not (a=b /\ b=c) => monochrome(a,b,c)=zero
  . plus(zero,zero)=zero
  . plus(zero,one)=one
  . plus(one,zero)=one
  . plus(one,one)=two
  . plus(zero,two)=two
  . plus(two,zero)=two
  . plus(plus(monochrome(a,b,c),monochrome(a,b,c)),cuts(a,b,c))=two
      %(original_paired_failure_plus_crossing_pairs)% %implied
end

%% All original CNFs: exact clocked transport to marked signed pair interactions.
%% A literal is an original or auxiliary bit with a sign. Keep a marked
%% anchor F=0. Write NAE(a,b,c) for not-all-equal. It is literally the
%% pair (a OR b OR c) and (not a OR not b OR not c); when the condition
%% fails, exactly one of the paired clauses fails (including repeated slots).
%% The three gates NAE(not a,u,F), NAE(not b,u,F), NAE(a,b,not u)
%% hold jointly iff u=a OR b. The first two require u if either input is
%% 1. When both are 0, the third forces u=0; when both are 1 it forces
%% u=1; when they differ it accepts either and the first two force u=1.
%% Build one OR chain per original clause, initially F. Give each original
%% output its own marked NAE(out,F,F), even for the empty clause. For each
%% original assignment x, the unique canonical gate lift L(x) is the OR
%% evaluation. It fails precisely the marked outputs of failed original
%% clauses, and no gates. The original projection and this lift take
%% linear word operations, with the literal sidecar (order, gate and clause
%% labels, pin, signs) retained. Reverse direction is on the verified
%% gate-consistent locus; no bijection with all auxiliary assignments.
%%
%% With m original clauses, let B=m+1. Give each gate constraint cost B and
%% each output cost 1. If A'(x,y) is this action, then
%%   min_y A'(x,y)=A_C(x), with unique minimizer L(x) above x.
%% A noncanonical extension fails at least one gate; its cost exceeds m.
%% Thus Z_C(z) is exactly the degree <= m truncation of the signed graph's
%% weight polynomial, also after any pins on the original variables.
%% Tropical elimination and low-degree projection are two routes through
%% the same marked translation. The latter retains original multiplicity,
%% not just the attainable minimum. For N translated bits and 2h>N, one
%% may instead evaluate at q=4^h and read the residue modulo q^(m+1):
%% each coefficient is <=2^N<q, so this reads all low coefficients with
%% zero carry between digits. The real quarter-gap ratio is then recovered
%% from those coefficients at z=4^-h. No claim that changing the base of
%% the address ultrametric itself computes an arithmetic residue.
%%
%% For a triple of literals with spins T_i=(-1)^(literal bit),
%%   4*[the triple fails NAE]=1+T_1*T_2+T_2*T_3+T_3*T_1.
%% This follows because the pair-product sum is 3 when all signs match,
%% and -1 otherwise. Multiply by the constraint cost and collect terms.
%% Write V=4 A'=K+sum h_i s_i+sum_(i<j) w_ij s_i s_j, s_i=(-1)^x_i.
%% Repeated variable slots give constants; the white anchor gives fields.
%% All weights are signed integers of magnitude polynomial in the written
%% CNF length (also their sum of absolute values is polynomial, since B=m+1).
%% Each pair acts in a 4x4 two-bit chart, with the s_i*s_j diagonal
%% (1,-1,-1,1). Negative literal signs change its coefficient, not the mark.
%% Equivalently in the Walsh chart this diagonal is conjugate to the
%% simultaneous two-bit flip by commuting the local transforms. Over Q_2
%% use unnormalized Walsh H: H^2=4I, so clear denominators rather than
%% treating H as an integral invertible frame. Native frame change reindexes
%% the retained chart and its source map together, preserving the scalar
%% and the original-clause return. The fixed native integral 4x4 frame
%% matrices and their inverses incur only linear bit cost per chart (and
%% frame words reduce using the six-frame multiplication table).
%% Signed 2-local data come from every written CNF, but they are not an
%% unsigned cut promise. The global maximum signed cut is not the
%% Padberg--Rao minimum odd cut; applying that algorithm here would
%% change the target. Nor is a theorem about an unmarked Ramanujan
%% adjacency spectrum a theorem about this marked action.
%%
%% Reverse proof return: if translated satisfiable, cost 0 forces all
%% gates and outputs; project the assignment. If translated coverage is
%% supplied as a false-constraint selector, feed in L(x) for each
%% challenged original x, so only originally labelled outputs can fail.
%% Prepending that label gives the Čech contraction on the original
%% infinite base. In the other direction an original negative selector
%% checks the gates of an arbitrary translated challenge, returning a
%% failed gate if it finds one; if none fail it uses the original
%% selector to name a failed output. This is polynomial overhead per
%% challenge, proof-bearing on both sides, and preserves the input query
%% with its original variables. It does not generate a general negative
%% selector from an unmarked scalar that lacks a certified sign.
%%
%% Signed class-field lift (including arbitrary unary fields):
%% Suppose after removing a supplied set J of k exceptional vertices the
%% remaining signed pair weights are constant on pairs of t vertex classes,
%% including off-diagonal pairs within a class. Assign J in 2^k ways. Pins
%% in J or any class simply add constants and unary fields and remove those
%% coordinates from the unassigned classes. At fixed counts a_i of negative
%% spins let S_i=v_i-2a_i. Then the pair contribution is
%%   sum_i w_ii (S_i^2-v_i)/2 + sum_(i<j) w_ij S_i S_j.
%% This is independent of which coordinates carry the negative spins.
%% Tropical route: for a class and count a_i the least unary energy is
%% sum h_r minus twice the sum of its a_i largest h_r, by a one-swap
%% inequality. Keep those exact variable labels for attaining assignments.
%% Bayes route: compute each class's count/energy table by multiplying
%% prod_r (z^h_r + u z^(-h_r)). A table entry counts precisely the sign
%% subsets of that size and unary energy, by coefficient induction. Adding
%% the pair shift at a fixed count tuple and combining the tables gives
%% the exact signed-action histogram. Only at the whole assignment total
%% action numerator do we divide by four; it is always nonnegative and
%% divisible by four. Integer magnitudes and total ranges are polynomial
%% in original source length here; histogram coefficients have O(N) bits.
%% At most (N+1)^t count tuples per exception assignment. Both routes
%% are polynomial for fixed t and k=O(log(input length)), with the given
%% J, including after original pins. They permit exact reconstruction of
%% original Z_C and R as above. No assumption that every signed graph
%% from the compiler has such a small interface. This is an additional
%% sufficient witness, not a general proof of P=NP.

spec MarkedSignedCutGate =
  free type CutBit ::= cut0 | cut1
  op cutOr : CutBit * CutBit -> CutBit
  op cutNot : CutBit -> CutBit
  pred cutNAE : CutBit * CutBit * CutBit
  vars a,b,u : CutBit
  . cutOr(cut0,b)=b
  . cutOr(cut1,b)=cut1
  . cutNot(cut0)=cut1
  . cutNot(cut1)=cut0
  . cutNAE(a,b,u) <=> not (a=b /\ b=u)
  . (cutNAE(cutNot(a),u,cut0) /\ cutNAE(cutNot(b),u,cut0) /\
      cutNAE(a,b,cutNot(u))) <=> u=cutOr(a,b)
      %(signed_cut_gate_unique_original_or)% %implied
  . cutNAE(a,cut0,cut0) <=> a=cut1
      %(signed_cut_output_returns_original_clause)% %implied
end

%% Hilbert24 assertion, translation and actual fold (finite marked aperture).
%% A proof path is a sequence of original marked cylinder branches and,
%% after signed-cut compilation, its auxiliary bits. This does not
%% identify the minimum failure count with a minimum over every possible
%% proof calculus: the grammar here is this declared marked path grammar.
%% Let V=4A'=K+Sum h_i*s_i+Sum_{i<j}w_ij*s_i*s_j be the weighted
%% signed-cut numerator from MarkedSignedCutGate. At a prefix P with
%% signs s_i, separate the incurred scalar cost from the residual:
%% f_j=h_j+Sum_{i in P}w_ij*s_i for each unread j. The unread-unread
%% weights remain the original weights; they depend only on the cut.
%% Thus the exact future, including all marked continuations, depends
%% only on the residual fields f, not the entire prefix. Retain the
%% original prefix on the path and keep the incurred scalar on its edges.
%% At next coordinate i, s=+/-1 has edge charge f_i*s and sends
%% f_j to f_j+w_ij*s for j>i. This follows by distributing the term
%% involving s_i through the unread interaction. Compose two such steps
%% for the four paracollatznormalized ports. Incoming paths sharing a
%% residual key share the future computation; they remain distinct paths.
%%
%% Let P(i,f;z) be the residual Laurent histogram in numerator units.
%% P(i,f;z)=sum_s z^(f_i*s) P(i+1,f';z), with terminal 1.
%% The Hilbert24 least-cost fold replaces sum by min and multiplication by
%% addition: E(i,f)=min_s(f_i*s+E(i+1,f')), terminal 0. Retain all
%% minimizing outgoing labels; choosing any path of those labels
%% produces an attaining original assignment, not merely its energy.
%% Both recurrences follow by partitioning the continuation fibre on
%% its first bit, an exact weighted pushforward, not a sampling estimate.
%% At the root shift exponents by K and divide them by four to recover
%% actual action grades. Only the shifted total exponents are guaranteed
%% nonnegative and divisible by four. Take two-bit steps when desired.
%% This is also the finite-temperature fold: evaluating the Laurent
%% histogram at exp(-beta/4) gives the unnormalized Gibbs sum, after
%% the root shift. With a probability prior mu on marked paths the free
%% energy is min_nu(E_nu c + KL(nu||mu)/beta), because the difference
%% from -log Z/beta is KL(nu||Gibbs)/beta. Use a full-support prior to
%% retain every tied minimizer. For uniform mu and S marked paths the
%% soft fold replaces min by -logsumexp/beta and adds log(S)/beta once.
%% The frame is an independent factor with Laplacian grades 0,2,3,3,5,5.
%% Divide out 1+z^2+2z^3+2z^5, using its unit constant coefficient.
%% Truncate to original grades <=m to erase every wrong auxiliary lift.
%% This restores exact multiplicities before evaluating the original
%% real free energy or Bayes ratio. The frame and gate operations are
%% distinct: integrating all inconsistent gate states at finite
%% temperature without truncation would in general change the prior.
%%
%% Budget theorem. The keys are (i,f), with sidecar ordered variable
%% labels. If S(C,order) keys are reached, both folds cost
%% S(C,order)*poly(|C|), for the compiled written CNF weights above.
%% Indeed the signed exponent range is bounded by the sum of absolute
%% unary and pair weights, which is polynomial, and each coefficient
%% has at most N+1 bits for N translated variables. The forward key
%% construction is by those same updates; no independent exponential
%% materialization of the cylinder tree is required.
%% At cut P|U the fields vary in h_U+W_(U,P)*{+1,-1}^P. If the
%% crossing matrix has rational rank r, choose r coordinate rows whose
%% projection is injective on its column span (Gaussian elimination).
%% Each selected varying integer coordinate is in [-M,M], with M
%% bounded by the max absolute row sum of the crossing matrix. Therefore
%% at most (2M+1)^r keys occur at that cut. For a supplied order with
%% uniformly bounded crossing rank, M=poly(|C|) makes the whole fold
%% polynomial. Exact more general guarantee is polynomial total keys,
%% not merely the existence of a constant frame gap. No uniform
%% polynomial bound on the keys for all written CNFs is asserted here.

spec H24ResidualFieldUpdate =
  sort Coeff
  ops plus, times : Coeff * Coeff -> Coeff
  vars h,w,s,t : Coeff
  . times(plus(h,w),t)=plus(times(h,t),times(w,t))
  . times(times(w,s),t)=times(w,times(s,t))
  . plus(times(h,t),times(w,times(s,t)))=times(plus(h,times(w,s)),t)
      %(h24_edge_update_preserves_energy_on_each_marked_completion)% %implied
end

%% Independent Hilbert24 read: gauge, min-cut, and a flow witness.
%% This computes the same original minimum without listing residual
%% states or computing the Bayes weight polynomial. For retained
%% interaction w_ij, seek gauge signs g_i with g_i*g_j=-sign(w_ij).
%% Propagation on a spanning forest and checking all remaining edges
%% is necessary and sufficient. Labels, addresses, OR gate penalties
%% and canonical lift are retained; undo a gauge by the same flip.
%% With s_i=g_i*t_i all residual pair terms become
%%   -|w_ij|*t_i*t_j = -|w_ij| + 2|w_ij|*[t_i!=t_j].
%% Unary H_i*t_i is min(H_i,-H_i) plus a nonnegative assignment
%% penalty (0 on the preferred sign, 2|H_i| on the other).
%% Therefore V=V0+cap(S), with one vertex per unread coordinate,
%% a symmetric pair edge of capacity 2|w_ij| and unary edges to the
%% marked source and sink. The cut S corresponds bijectively to signs.
%% Flow conservation summed over S gives flow value <= cap(S).
%% A flow and a cut with equal value certify the exact minimum. Decode
%% the cut to a marked spin assignment, undo gauge, project to original
%% coordinates. Every original challenged assignment on the negative
%% rail has its canonical lift; the lower bound forces a failed original
%% output on that lift. Its label gives the Cech contraction vertex.
%%
%% With k exceptional vertices, branch on their retained bits. Terms
%% meeting those vertices are constants/unary after pinning. If the
%% residual pair graph is gauge-balanced, all 2^k cylinders are handled
%% by flows; dual bounds cover all branches, and the cheapest cut attains
%% the original Hilbert24 minimum. Clock 2^k*poly(original input).
%% The backdoor need not be supplied. In a graph of sign constraints,
%% use one ordinary edge to require opposite colours, and a two-edge
%% path with a fresh middle vertex to require equal colours. After
%% deleting original vertices, a bipartite colouring exists iff the
%% surviving sign constraints are consistent; a path with one endpoint
%% deleted imposes no constraint. If an ordinary odd-cycle transversal
%% deletes a middle vertex, replace that deletion by deleting one of
%% its original endpoints. Reinstall the middle vertex with degree at
%% most one, so bipartiteness is preserved and size does not increase.
%% Thus ordinary odd-cycle transversal finds a sign backdoor of the
%% same optimal size, in 2^O(k)*poly(original input), via known FPT OCT.
%% It suffices to search up to the required logarithmic cap, returning
%% OPEN for this route if the cap is exceeded. Degree and crossing rank
%% play no role in this bound. This is not a bound on general backdoor
%% size and is not the same graph problem as minimum odd T-cut.
%%
%% A useful partial return on EVERY marked input, without a backdoor:
%% choose a spanning-forest gauge. For each remaining positive pair
%% penalty +J*t_i*t_j, retain the constant -J but drop the nonnegative
%% 2J*[t_i=t_j]. Solve the resulting ordinary cut. Its flow provides a
%% sound lower bound on V for all original lifts. Its attaining cut,
%% checked against the unchanged original energy, provides an upper
%% bound (the cost of a specified feasible assignment). If the lower
%% bound is positive, return certified UNSAT for the original program.
%% If the cut projects to a checked original model, return SAT. If
%% neither happens, retain that gap as open (do not treat it as false).
%% An exact minimum or either sound decision already produces the same
%% quarter-accurate tower read via 2 on SAT and 1 on UNSAT. This does not
%% claim a quarter-accurate probability/count evaluation on SAT with
%% any other normalization; the tower and existing range-gap Bayes
%% decision can each use their own valid endpoint approximants.
%%
%% Sources for the network and parameterized subroutines:
%% https://www.robots.ox.ac.uk/~cvrg/trinity2005/kolmogorov_zabih_pami04.pdf
%% https://arxiv.org/abs/1203.0833

%% All-length clock normalization of the original missed-deadline question.
%% Reuses the actual saved source blocks WTBConcreteMachineReturns,
%% WTBConcreteSharedWitnessReturns and CJClockedReturns in
%% Final_Proofs_Seven_Twenty_GRH_YM_Hilbert24_PNP.het, and the
%% CollatzTailHaarReturns / CanonicalStoredProofName branch of
%% Canonical_Collatz_Programme_Bridge.het. These are witness transport
%% theorems, not axioms asserting that the candidate machine is correct.
%%
%% FIX one witness-producing machine M and one timeout p including
%% output verification. Valid length-N written formulas are N-bit
%% records C. Canonically relabel at most N distinct variables in order
%% of first occurrence; pad the model bits to an N-bit record a and
%% require the unused bits to be zero. Invalid records are masked out.
%% Run the actual wrapper through the deadline and read the bit h_C
%% saying a checked original model was returned before or at the cutoff.
%% This is only a bounded run; on any original input the wrapper decides
%% YES if h_C=1 and NO otherwise, and never accepts falsely.
%% In the normalized two-bit chart (v,h) let v=[C(a)=1] (also checking
%% valid record/padding). The missed-clock projector is exactly
%% d(C,a)=v*(1-h_C). Its only marked live port is (1,0); other ports zero.
%% At each finite aperture the clock test is thus the original CNF
%% testing gates and the single live port. Canonical signed-cut lifts
%% make this equivalence pointwise. Address the payload by the ordinary
%% Collatz parity cylinder of its self-delimiting bit word, not by
%% the machine run's working-copy state. Reading back yields the original
%% (C,a) plus the timed run, via deterministic simulation on that input.
%%
%% Let b_N=sum_(C,a) d(C,a). Then 0<=b_N<=2^(2N).
%% The local min-plus version takes min of 1-d(C,a): zero means a miss.
%% The outer Boolean version asks b_N>0, so zero means no miss.
%% The same b_N is the constant-in-energy coefficient of the marked
%% bounded counterexample generating function. Form the single dyadic
%% normalized object Xi=Sum_(N>=0) 2^(N*N)*b_N in Z_2.
%% Width of the length-N block is (N+1)^2-N^2=2N+1, so NO carries
%% can enter an adjacent block, even at maximum b_N. Restriction
%% modulo 2^(k*k) retains exactly lengths N<k. Projection to length N
%% reads the residue of floor(the canonical residue mod 2^((N+1)^2)
%% divided by 2^(N*N)), not the leading magnitude in the real metric.
%% Conversely those compatible finite readings reconstruct Xi by the
%% inverse limit, no compactness search. Paracollatz reindexing is a
%% labelled bijection on cylinders, hence preserves b_N and Xi.
%% If Xi != 0 and its first nonzero digit is k, the first failed length
%% is floor(sqrt(k)); the block has positive count. For a concrete
%% countermodel descend by the exact finite child counts, choosing a
%% positive child at each split; this is a correctness construction
%% without a polynomial claim for computing those child counts.
%% Xi=0 iff there is no original missed deadline for this same M,p.
%% Therefore P=NP iff some M and polynomial p have Xi(M,p)=0:
%% the forward implication uses the usual search-to-decision reductions,
%% fixing original variables one at a time; the reverse uses the
%% time-bounded checked-output decision wrapper on the original CNF.
%% Z_2 identifies zero by all its cylinder restrictions. This is the
%% normalized full assertion (same candidate on every cylinder),
%% not a proof that a specified candidate vanishes in every restriction.
%% A uniform terminal proof may use any sound method whose conclusion
%% is that there are no live ports (1,0), on the actual labelled run.

spec ClockMissedPort =
  free type ClockBit ::= c0 | c1
  op missClock : ClockBit * ClockBit -> ClockBit
  vars v,h : ClockBit
  . missClock(c0,h)=c0
  . missClock(v,c1)=c0
  . missClock(c1,c0)=c1
  . (missClock(v,h)=c1) <=> (v=c1 /\ h=c0)
      %(clock_only_original_model_and_missed_deadline_port_survives)% %implied
end

%% Amortization on the paracollatznormalized torser: whole clock, not warm reads.
%% For the residual-field fold, retain a set R of discovered normalized
%% continuation keys, a pending subset P, all labelled incoming branches,
%% and the decoded result register. A two-bit expansion consumes one pending
%% key and discovers at most four fresh keys. Cache hits retain their
%% marks, and are counted within the paying expansion. Bound the bit
%% cost of one expansion, including all searches, key construction,
%% updates, and exact polynomial-size arithmetic by q(input length),
%% using a deterministic trie on bounded-length normalized keys.
%% Process live keys and evaluate in decreasing aperture order (or use
%% a separate completion pass). Reserve credit q at discovery for both
%% passes, taking q large enough for their combined cost. Charge input
%% setup and final read separately. This statement is for the ordinary
%% bit machine (or a separately costed reversible simulation).
%% Admission of b new keys issues b*q; processing one pays from its
%% credit, and reuse issues none. In the single-pass accounting view
%% P means all discovered work not yet paid out. Potential Phi=q*|P|.
%% If W is actual cost, then W_step+Phi_next-Phi_now <= b*q. Thus
%% W_total+Phi_final <= Phi_initial + q*(number of fresh keys).
%% This is the earlier Hilbert24 reserve telescoping applied to actual
%% paracollatznormalized continuations. All occurrences are marked and
%% the same key is charged only once. Restricting to two-bit ports or
%% transporting the frame with the marks preserves the cost proof.
%% With an initially empty cache and the charge of the root included,
%% the exact amortized construction is polynomial on the proved
%% polynomial-key families. If all reachable keys are materialized,
%% the number of keys itself is a lower bound; no cost potential can
%% remove the cost of a genuinely new key in that implementation.
%%
%% All-orders obstruction to a global bound for this precise key rule:
%% Let v=2^d and use the Boolean cube Q_d as the interaction graph,
%% distinct from the fixed six-frame Ramanujan torser. In original
%% variables use both implication clauses for each edge equality.
%% Their exact source action is A=sum_edges [s_i!=s_j].
%% This is already two-local: each edge has one marked 4x4 chart,
%% so no gate auxiliaries are needed. In 4A the pair weights are -2.
%% Take the balanced middle cut of ANY fixed variable order, including
%% an order read in pairs when d>=2. The indicator spin vector of this
%% cut has mean zero and squared norm v. The nonzero cube Laplacian
%% eigenvalues are 2|S| by parity characters, so 4|edges crossing|>=2v.
%% Greedily choose a crossing edge (a,b). To get a crossing induced
%% matching, discard every edge (i,j) for which (a,j) OR (i,b) crosses.
%% Each choice discards at most 2d^2 edges (neighbors times degree),
%% so the matching has at least v/(4d^2) pairs. Fix all earlier spins
%% except the matching's earlier endpoints. At each corresponding later
%% endpoint, its residual unary field changes with exactly the matched
%% earlier endpoint among those still varying: the diagonal weights
%% are -2 and the off-diagonal weights are zero. Thus the normalized
%% residual vectors distinguish all 2^matching choices on this one cut.
%% This is superpolynomial in the written input length O(v*d^2).
%% Hence fully expanding the exact field-key graph cannot have a
%% uniform polynomial clock on these presentations, regardless of
%% amortization or fixed variable order. A solver is NOT required to use
%% that expansion. The very same energy is gauge-balanced and the
%% native flow result (or direct equality propagation for the zero
%% question) returns it in polynomial time. This lower bound is for
%% this specified fold, not for other torser proofs or SAT algorithms.

spec ClockAmortizedPotentialStep =
  sort Credit
  ops plus, stateCredit : Credit * Credit -> Credit
  vars q,remaining,issued,spent : Credit
  . stateCredit(q,plus(remaining,issued))=
    plus(stateCredit(q,remaining),stateCredit(q,issued))
  . plus(spent,stateCredit(q,plus(remaining,issued)))=
    plus(spent,plus(stateCredit(q,remaining),stateCredit(q,issued)))
      %(clock_credit_for_old_pending_work_and_fresh_states_separates)% %implied
end
19. Bayes and Hilbert 24 on the same original evidence

Complete original source: Bayes_Hilbert24_Native_Read.zip :: Bayes_Hilbert24_Native_Read/README.md

grade convention fixed to checked original model n+1 and unconditional fallback n+2 posterior on specified accepted tagged experiment, not generic model distribution uniform absolute error <=1/16 for rho; threshold 1/8 credit nonnegative at each step; original total work debited; one polynomial bound on initial credit automatic acquisition proved for original 2-CNF; general CNF may use supplied original model or accepted resolution proof

tagged counts (1,M,M,M) and rho=M/(1+3M); minimum grade n+1 iff SAT, fallback n+2; bounded 1/16 posterior reader iff P=NP; native integral verdict modulo 8; reserve telescoping including acquisition/verification/transport/readback; original labelled 2-CNF SCC/resolution constructor

# Bayes and Hilbert 24 on the same original evidence

## Start with the six-port ring

Use the supplied ring at aperture \(1/4\), with one seam of cost \(81/400\) and the other five of cost zero. With independent fair bonds, condition on all six ports being connected. Exactly seven bond masks survive: every single omission and the full rim. Clearing the cost denominator by 400 gives one mask of grade zero and six of grade 81. For positive weight parameter \(t\le1\), the conditional partition function is
\[
 Z_{\rm conn}(t)=1+6t^{81}.
\]
The full rim carries phase \(-1\), and its posterior weight in this marked family is \(t^{81}/Z_{\rm conn}(t)\), equal to \(1/7\) at \(t=1\). The Hilbert 24 minimum returns the unique grade-zero mask, whose five-bond path closes through the apex with phase \(+1\). The loop mark remains part of the evidence.

## The original CNF packet

Let \(C\) be the original CNF, with \(n\) occurring original variables and model count \(M\). Give the always-available fallback grade \(n+2\) and every checked original satisfying assignment grade \(n+1\). This is the existing Hilbert 24 question. The Bayesian tags only repeat the original witnesses and inherit their grade.

Choose \(n\) fair assignment bits and two fair tag bits. Tag \(00\) admits exactly the all-zero bit string as the fallback. Each of \(01,10,11\) admits an original assignment exactly when it passes the original clauses. The accepted marked counts are \((1,M,M,M)\). The joint evidence likelihood is \((1+3M)/2^{n+2}\). After conditioning, the one-tag read and the read of any marked tag are
\[
 \rho=\frac{M}{1+3M},\qquad B=3\rho.
\]
The exact read requires information about the count, since \(M=\rho/(1-3\rho)\). The tagged partition function is
\[
 t^{n+2}+3Mt^{n+1}.
\]
Its first occupied degree is \(n+1\) precisely when the original CNF is SAT, and otherwise is \(n+2\). The grade-minimizing model is read on the same marked packet. If \(t=e^{-\beta}\) and \(\beta>0\), the free energy is between the minimum grade minus \(\log(1+3M)/\beta\) and the minimum grade. This follows by comparing each positive weight with that of a minimum.

For the separate soft gate based on all original assignments, write \(A_C(a)\) for the number of original clauses failed by \(a\) and \(Z_C(t)=\sum_a t^{A_C(a)}\). At \(t=0\) the grade-zero coefficient is \(M\). With a unit fallback, the one-tag posterior for soft weights is \(Z_C(t)/(1+3Z_C(t))\). Since \(0\le Z_C(t)-M\le 2^n t\) for \(0<t\le1\), taking \(t=2^{-(n+6)}\) gives posterior at most \(1/67\) on UNSAT and at least \(1/4\) on SAT. This is a separate positive-weight realization of the same support threshold.

## The cheap certified read

| Read | UNSAT | SAT | Sufficient uniform absolute error |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| One tag \(\rho\) | \(0\) | \([1/4,1/3)\) | \(1/16\), threshold \(1/8\) |
| Any marked tag \(B\) | \(0\) | \([3/4,1)\) | \(1/4\), threshold \(3/8\) |
| Native terminal code | \(0\bmod8\) | \(4\bmod8\) | \(2\)-adic perturbation of norm at most \(1/8\) |

Given a checked original model, \(9/32\) approximates \(\rho\) with error at most \(5/96<1/16\), and \(27/32\) approximates \(B\) within \(1/4\). A checked original refutation returns 0 for both hard reads. The same estimates meet the error bounds for the soft gate at the chosen \(t\), since its UNSAT one-tag value lies in \([0,1/67]\). This avoids evaluating the model count. The posterior is a rational observable. The native verdict uses the integral scalar matrix \(4bI_4\). Since the Gram dagger fixes the identity, paracollatznormalization takes this scalar to \(-4bI_4\), congruent to \(4bI_4\bmod8\). The compiler checks all six native frame charts. The original mark and its checker remain attached.

A proposed deterministic uniform \(1/16\) reader of \(\rho\) settles SAT by threshold comparison. Conversely, a polynomial SAT decider computes the stated estimate in polynomial time. This equivalence concerns the bounded read. With positive verdicts, successive original pins recover an actual model. A particular compact refutation language has its own proof-production requirement.

If one fixed producer acquires the certified read with nonnegative credit \(\Phi\), per-step work \(c_i\), and
\[
  c_i+\Phi_{i+1}\le\Phi_i,\qquad \Phi_0\le p(|C|),
\]
then summing the inequalities gives the requisite uniform bound on that original input. Include acquisition, verification, native transport, and readback in the debit. Uniform \(p\) is the remaining all-CNF obligation. Rejection sampling directly from the two-tag-bit experiment takes expected \(2^{n+2}/(1+3M)\) trials for one accepted draw, so that method has exponential instances.

## A working restricted producer

For original 2-CNF, the implementation builds the implication graph with original clause labels. A contradictory strongly connected component gives two marked paths \(x\leadsto\neg x\) and \(\neg x\leadsto x\). Resolving along these paths derives opposite units and then the empty clause. The same resolution checker from the Čech witness validates each step and reads one original failed clause from any challenged assignment. If there is no such component, choose each literal according to the topological order of its component versus its negation and check the resulting original assignment. This yields one checked support bit with a graph and a resolution receipt of linear size. The full read runs in polynomial original bit work.

The supplied-packet read also handles general CNF with either an actual original model or an accepted original resolution proof. Automatic acquisition outside 2-CNF is outside this producer's proved domain.

    python3 ring_bayes.py --t 1
    python3 ring_bayes.py --t 1/2
    python3 bayes_hilbert.py examples/sat.json --include-certificate
    python3 bayes_hilbert.py examples/unsat.json --include-certificate

E proved ten local CASL goals. Hets kept them marked open. Lean independently checked the quarter band and the integer-cleared \(9/32\) error bound, with the propext dependency reported. The formal replay scope is the local arithmetic, support and guard laws.
20. Exact native source generator and complete original certificate production

Complete original source: Hilbert24_ConstructMinimum_Fixed.zip :: Hilbert24_ConstructMinimum/runtime/Construct_Minimum_Runtime_Notes.txt

Same original Boolean verifier, retained original source marks, declared coefficient representation and separately charged complete evaluation work.

source-indexed constructor; original-source checker; normalized four-port update

DIRECT HILBERT 24 CONSTRUCTOR: EXECUTABLE SOURCE RETURN

The entry point is constructMinimum(original_clauses, declared_n). It takes
the original CNF alone and returns an OptimalReturn containing an original
model or the admitted fallback, its accumulated proof grade, the actual
source-constructor rule chain, the cofactor identities, and local zero-slack
evidence. It does not call the previous integral/counting/tag executors.

1. WHAT NOW DRIVES THE COMPUTATION

TightSourcePolicy.chosen chooses one source-indexed rule at each goal. A
live goal contains its remaining original width, a source-compiled position,
and dependent coefficient factors. A two-coordinate rule has ONE premise:
the source family after its exact four-port pushforward. A final unpaired
coordinate uses the genuine two-port family. A compressed free-coordinate
rule removes exactly the original declared unused coordinates.

The chosen rule at positive width does not read the source SAT bit. It
constructs the next coefficient family. Only the zero-coordinate leaf reads
its already-constructed constant coefficient and selects model_leaf or
fallback_leaf. The generic one-child recursion is implemented with an
explicit stack so Python's recursion depth is not a source restriction.

The return traverses the same rules in reverse. In the positive branch it
selects an attaining port under the already-fixed remaining assignment and
recovers the original removed bits. In the negative branch it retains the
whole cofactor-cover return, so zero of the joined family yields falsity on
every original branch. The final positive assignment has an explicit zero
default for every original coordinate not set to one.

Rule charge is the number of removed original coordinates. A model leaf has
charge 1 and a fallback leaf charge 2. constructMinimum adds these charges
as the dependent return is constructed. The grade is this accumulated sum:
n+1 or n+2, exactly the original fixed-verifier candidate grade.

2. THE CALIBRATION IS A SPECIFICATION, NOT A ROOT ANSWER ORACLE

The symbolic potential of a source goal q is

  V(q)=width(q)+(1 if the source family has a model else 2).

The exact source pushforward preserves the source family's existential
truth. Hence charge(rule)+V(premise)=V(conclusion), with zero slack. Width
strictly decreases along every nonterminal rule. The concrete terminal
constructor attains its displayed potential.

No live potential value is evaluated before construction. The tests replace
SourceCalibration.potential by a function that raises immediately if called;
all original-source runs still complete. The local proof checker validates
the actual cofactor and source-cover identities that justify tightness,
rather than trusting the words 'zero slack' in a return packet.

The accompanying Lean development instantiates the existing calibrated
constructMinimum with the corresponding source RuleSystem and proves the
whole original candidate minimum. The Python implementation is checked
separately by its local certificate replay and independent source tests.

3. COEFFICIENT FAMILIES AND THE NATIVE NORMALIZED OPERATION

GroundFamilies is the source-function algebra with values {0,infinity}.
Zero means a valid local condition; infinity means its fibre is empty.
Pointwise best is minimum, and pointwise serial composition is addition.
All finite local values are zero, so these are exactly Boolean OR and AND
on presence. Original source dependencies remain in the literal leaves.
There is no numerical model-count circuit to construct or interpret.

Every bucket includes all factors mentioning either eliminated coordinate.
Only factors independent of that block remain outside. Thus no independent
scalar support is substituted for a dependent factor: the x AND NOT x
case remains zero. Clause tautologies and duplicates are simplified only
as original Boolean conditions, not as weighted proof multiplicities.

For each pair, the runtime actually executes the sparse 4 by 4 J L J
contraction in the ground semiring. It also calls native_normalization.py
on the exact ordered coefficient-node marks. That helper executes N_U,
the transported operator Q_N=N_U Q (I-2U), and the inverse original read.
Its coefficients are ordinary rational linear forms with odd denominators,
in the admitted integral 2-adic module. N_U is never applied to min-plus
values. Ground projection occurs after the positive original port sum is
returned. The normalized forms and their actual operation counters are
part of each four-port certificate. The actually computed positive original
readback supplies the returned ground family. The separate native J L J
ground operation is checked against that same value under associative Best
normalization; it is not the sole data path with normalization on the side.

Sharing preserves the original port alternatives. A repeated original port
keeps its multiplicity in the ordinary normalized linear packet, although
minimum is idempotent and may share its ground value. Reverse selectors
still use the ordered original ports.

4. SOURCE SCHEDULE AND WORK ACCOUNTING

The schedule is compiled once: increasing original incidence count, with
ties in first source occurrence order. Consecutive coordinates are paired.
It never scores all residual variable candidates at every cut. This makes
the private x_i,y_i pairs precede the common separator in the saved
attachment examples. This is a stated deterministic source schedule, not
a claim that its producer runtime is minimal among all possible schedules.

For the 129-variable, 128-clause positive k=64 source, the new constructor
acquires 451 ground nodes. It performs 64 pair rules, one unary rule, and
one model leaf: 66 chosen rules and 66 dependent return applications.
The final original candidate has grade 130. There are no residual-order
candidate scans, no exact counts, no live-potential reads, and one terminal
coefficient read. The normalized native operation runs on all 64 pairs.

For the 131-variable, 136-clause negative cube-core source, it acquires 466
ground nodes, constructs 67 rules/returns, executes 65 normalized pair
operations, and returns the fallback with grade 133. Its negative source
certificate is checked by the original-coordinate cover and every local
cofactor identity. It is not accepted merely because a flag says false.

These candidate grades are exact constructor-derivation charges. Actual
coefficient acquisition, set/dictionary operations, rational normalization,
selector observation, and optional proof serialization/checking are real
execution work. Their structural counters are reported separately. One
warm constructor timing is included per example, expressly excluding
Python startup, test-oracle work, and independent certificate replay.
No all-CNF polynomial acquired-family bound is claimed.

5. INDEPENDENT PROOF CHECKER

verify_minimum.py imports neither the producer nor a SAT solver. It checks:

  - the original clause indicators and original coordinate domain;
  - the admitted acyclic ground-algebra constructors;
  - every source cofactor identity by its structural homomorphism;
  - that each bucket contains every factor dependent on its source block;
  - the exact ordered 2/4-port source cover and the actual J L J nesting;
  - the N_U, transported contraction, and original marked readback forms;
  - the connected decreasing-width constructor chain and local charges;
  - the terminal coefficient, original candidate branch, and exact grade;
  - the positive assignment against the unreduced original clauses.

The negative proof follows the checked complete source cover, without
enumerating assignments. OptimalReturn.reject applies that universal
negative return to a supplied original assignment and reports an original
failed-clause index. Small tests independently exercise this application
on every assignment of their negative fixtures.

6. REPRODUCTION

  python3 construct_minimum.py examples/attachment_free_64.cnf --grade --verify
  python3 construct_minimum.py examples/attachment_negative_cube_64.cnf --grade --verify
  python3 check_construct_minimum.py

Expected grades are 130 and 133. The default command prints only the
original SAT bit, after actually constructing the OptimalReturn. Optional
--certificate PATH retains the complete source proof packet; --stats PATH
retains operation counts. --verify independently replays that packet before
printing the result. There is no call to an old solver hidden in this path.

The test report records 747 independent original-source cases, 776 local
certificate replays, 11 rejected proof mutations, and 28 attachment family
instances. Old solver/counting modules are replaced by objects that raise
on access during the tests. A second trap corrupts the computed normalized
readback and confirms that the constructor rejects it, so normalization
cannot be an ignored side calculation. Nested Best families use associative
and idempotent normalization, checked independently, without distributing
products or expanding complete assignments. The original ten-million-free-coordinate source
uses two ground nodes and an explicit compressed free-coordinate rule;
its constructed grade is 10,000,001, with no enormous count computed.

The finite exhaustive oracle belongs only to the test file. The constructor
and the certificate checker impose no test-size, proof-depth, node-budget,
or coefficient-precision cap.
21. Reversibility, actual-work induction, and dependent adjoints

Complete original source: Hilbert24_ConstructMinimum_Fixed.zip :: Hilbert24_ConstructMinimum/Reversibility_Time_Adjoints.txt

An actual indexed trace with the supplied local balance, original retained input and output, exact dependent fibre equalities, and the declared cost per operation.

credit_telescopes; actual_work_bound; cleanup_cost; sigma_pull_adjunction; pull_pi_adjunction

REVERSIBLE CONSTRUCTION, ELAPSED WORK, AND DEPENDENT ADJUNCTIONS
============================================================

This extension applies to the repaired H24 constructor. It provides an
executed local reversible four-port cell and a separately checked logical
and resource interface. The complete Python source compiler has not been
translated into a reversible gate machine by this extension.

1. THE COMPUTATIONAL STATE MUST RETAIN THE ORIGINAL INPUT

Fredkin and Toffoli give concrete invertible primitives [R1]:

  Fredkin(c,a,b) = (c,a,b) if c=0, and (c,b,a) if c=1;
  Toffoli(a,b,c) = (a,b,c XOR (a AND b)).

Both are involutions. Fredkin also preserves the number of one-bits;
Toffoli does not generally preserve that number. Reversibility and this
additional conservation property are separate statements.

The four-port presence map (p00,p01,p10,p11) -> OR(ports) loses information.
The implemented reversible lift instead has eight wires and acts as

  (ports,b,0,0,0) -> (ports,b XOR OR(ports),0,0,0).

For two input bits a,b and an initially zero target t, execute

  CNOT(a,t), CNOT(b,t), Toffoli(a,b,t).

The target becomes a XOR b XOR ab = a OR b. Apply three such cells to
obtain the OR of four inputs: nine gates. Copy its value into the output
with one CNOT, then execute those nine gates in reverse order. The three
scratch bits return to zero. The original four port values remain present.

The actual adapter uses 13 CNOT and six Toffoli gates, for nineteen steps.
All 32 clean input/output combinations pass. The full eight-wire circuit
is a permutation on all 256 states, and every inverse return is checked.
The Fredkin decomposition CNOT(a,b), Toffoli(c,b,a), CNOT(a,b) is checked
on all eight inputs. The receipt records every gate and intermediate state
for a representative 38-step forward/inverse return.

This is a concrete instance of Bennett's compute, retain the output, and
uncompute pattern [R2]. For a reversible forward circuit with G elementary
gates and m classical bits retained by CNOT, the corresponding unit-gate
cost is 2G+m. Our one-output cell has G=9 and m=1. The cost of obtaining
the four dependent input readings is outside that local gate count.

2. WHY TIME CANNOT BE FORGOTTEN IN THE RESOURCE STATEMENT

On data states, g^{-1}g is the identity. An execution that actually applies
both factors nevertheless uses c(g)+c(g^{-1}) charged work. The positive
serial clock in reversible_four_port.py records this distinction: nineteen
steps for the cell, then nineteen more for its inverse. Algebraic identity
on the data is compatible with a nonzero execution grade.

An H24 simplifier can remove an inverse pair before it is executed, thereby
reducing future work. This is different from deleting the cost of a pair
already executed. A proof-loop quotient must therefore specify whether it
preserves source meaning, proof grade, or the actual historical work trace.

The current constructor's candidate grade n+1/n+2 measures the stated
original proof representation. Actual execution includes coefficient
acquisition, source substitution, normalization, retention, selector
readback, and any cleanup that is performed. Each needs a declared cost
model. Unit reversible-gate counts and measured Python wall times are
different measurements and must not be added as if they had the same units.

3. THE CHECKED AMORTIZATION LAW

For each transition, let c be actual work, c_hat its amortized charge, and
Psi_before/Psi_after nonnegative retained resource credit. Supply the balance

  c + Psi_after = c_hat + Psi_before.

The type CreditTrace before after requires adjacent credits to agree.
The new Lean module proves, by induction over this actual trace,

  actualWork + finalCredit = amortizedWork + initialCredit,
  actualWork <= amortizedWork + initialCredit.

It also proves the bound obtained when amortizedWork and initialCredit
are independently bounded. No root SAT answer appears in this theorem.
Credit must be supplied by an accounted acquisition or initial reserve;
assigning a large uncharged initial credit is not a runtime saving.

The concrete checked trace has:

  step       actual   credit before   credit after   amortized
  first         7           5              2             4
  second        3           2              1             2

Hence 10+1=6+5=11. The value six is the amortized charge; actual work is ten.
The formal cleanup ledger separately proves G+m+G=2G+m.

Reuse and reversible checkpointing can change the amount stored versus
recomputed. Bennett's reversible pebbling theorem gives explicit time/space
trade-offs for that choice [R3]. For this producer, a useful resource policy
would assign credit to retained coefficient families, their shared uses,
and their scheduled cleanup, then prove the displayed balances for each
actual operation. This extension supplies the checked accounting interface;
it does not assert an all-input coefficient-acquisition bound.

4. TRIPLE ADJOINTS KEEP THE CONDITIONS ON THE EVIDENCE

For f:X->Y, the family operations are

  (Sigma_f A)_y = sum_{x in f^{-1}(y)} A_x,
  (f^* B)_x = B_{f(x)},
  (Pi_f A)_y = product_{x in f^{-1}(y)} A_x.

Their adjunctions are Sigma_f left adjoint to f^*, and f^* left adjoint
to Pi_f [R4]. In proof-bearing families, the sum retains a source point
and the proof above it; the product supplies evidence at every source
point. On predicates, these become existential quantification, substitution,
and universal quantification. Adjunction units/counits need not be inverses.

Hilbert24_Adjoints_Amortization.lean proves the predicate adjunctions with
pointwise implication, explicit source equalities, and no choice or
extensionality axioms. It also proves the fibre-context/Frobenius law:

  Sigma_f(P AND f^*Q)(y) iff (Sigma_f P)(y) AND Q(y).

This is the precise permission to leave a factor outside a coordinate cut:
the factor must already be pulled back from the remaining context. The
runtime puts all factors depending on either removed coordinate inside
the same bucket. Shared dependencies are therefore preserved before the
existential pushforward.

At an actual four-port projection, a true existential coefficient has an
attaining original port. The stored selector returns that port and its
evidence. A false existential coefficient rejects all four ports; iteration
returns the universal original rejection. Reversible state retention and
these adjunctions serve complementary roles: one retains recoverable machine
data, the other specifies the dependent meaning of the proof returned.

22. Native graph minimum, closed integral, and exact spectral adapters

Complete original source: Hilbert24_ConstructMinimum_Fixed.zip :: Hilbert24_ConstructMinimum/graphs/Graph_Spectral_Constructor.txt

The supplied finite native carrier and marked source objective; the source retains its exact sparsification, coefficient, and original-return assumptions.

native graph minimum constructor; relative-sparsification kernel consequence; closed path integral

# Native graph construction and the spectral route to H24

The concrete correction is a constructor on the actual six-frame carrier. It
computes a minimum word from the relative group element and returns the entire
minimum fibre, including ties. It does not generate paths and then compare them.
The graph papers explain how to pursue this same pattern at larger scale:
compute an invariant algebraically, select a successor certified by that
invariant, and preserve the original marked return throughout.

## 1. An actual minimum constructor on the recovered native carrier

Use the recovered sparse matrix, rather than a replacement permutation model:

\[
\rho(\tau,b,L)=
\begin{pmatrix}
\tau-2b&0&0&0\\
0&L_{00}&L_{01}&0\\
0&L_{10}&L_{11}&0\\
b&0&0&\tau
\end{pmatrix},\qquad
S=\begin{pmatrix}-1&1\\0&1\end{pmatrix},\quad
T=\begin{pmatrix}1&0\\1&-1\end{pmatrix}.
\]

The six group blocks are \(I,S,T,ST,TS,STS\); the full matrix of a frame is
\(M_g=\rho(1,\operatorname{parity}(g),L_g)\). For the recovered Gram matrix

\[
H=\begin{pmatrix}1&0&0&1\\0&2&-1&0\\0&-1&2&0\\1&0&0&2\end{pmatrix}
\]

the check establishes \(M_g^{\dagger_H}=H^{-1}M_g^tH=M_{g^{-1}}\) and
\(M_gM_h=M_{gh}\). All coefficients needed for this dagger are integral with
possible denominator 3, hence are valid over \(\mathbb Z_2\).

Admit all three reflections \(\mathcal R=\{S,T,STS\}\), each with unit grade.
For source \(a\) and target \(b\), put \(h=a^{-1}b\). The constructor is

\[
\operatorname{MinWords}(a,b)=
\begin{cases}
\{[]\},&h=e,\\
\{[h]\},&h\text{ is a reflection},\\
\{[r,rh]:r\in\mathcal R\},&h\text{ is a nonidentity rotation}.
\end{cases}
\]

In the last case, \(rh\) is again a reflection and \(r(rh)=h\). This directly
constructs all three attaining two-step words. The three cases are group
classification; there is no chosen proof-depth limit.

Define the calibration

\[
V_b(a)=\begin{cases}0&a=b,\\1&a^{-1}b\text{ odd},\\2&\text{otherwise}.
\end{cases}
\]

Every admitted edge \(a\to ar\) has nonnegative slack
\(\sigma_b(a,r)=1+V_b(ar)-V_b(a)\). Every edge selected by the constructor has
zero slack and lowers the rank \(V_b\). For every competitor path of length
\(k\) from \(a\) to \(b\), telescoping gives

\[
k=V_b(a)+\sum_{\text{edge occurrences}}\sigma_b(a_i,r_i)\ge V_b(a).
\]

The constructed word has equality. This is the H24 calibration/zero-slack
construction theorem instantiated on the actual frame graph, with explicit
policy and rank. For a constructed word \(w\), the same native matrix product
returns \(M_aM_w=M_b\). The inverse returns every original marked packet:
\(\rho(v)M_wM_w^{\dagger_H}=\rho(v)\).

The script checks all 36 source/target pairs, all 60 attaining words, 108 local
edge inequalities, and 360 returns of the six-coordinate packet basis. Checking
a basis proves the linear packet identity for arbitrary coefficients. The
constructor has constant algebraic work per frame pair; any coefficient
arithmetic cost still depends on the representation of those coefficients.
This grade is the stated unit grade on frame operations. Its identification
with another source judgement's proof grade requires the corresponding map.

## 2. Why Cayley and Ramanujan are relevant here

Every even element of \(S_3\) is adjacent to every odd element, so the graph is
exactly \(K_{3,3}\). In this bipartition ordering,

\[
A=\begin{pmatrix}0&J_3\\J_3&0\end{pmatrix},\quad L=3I-A,
\quad \operatorname{spec}(A)=\{3,-3,0,0,0,0\}.
\]

The nontrivial adjacency eigenvalues are all zero, satisfying the degree-three
Ramanujan bound. This provides exact finite functional calculus:

\[
A^3=9A,\qquad
P_+=\frac{A^2+3A}{18}=\frac{J_6}{6},\qquad
P_-=\frac{A^2-3A}{18},\qquad P_0=I-\frac{A^2}{9}.
\]

These are orthogonal idempotents of ranks 1, 1, and 4. In particular,

\[
P_{\ker L}=\frac{(L-3I)(L-6I)}{18},\qquad
L^+=\frac{7L-L^2}{36}.
\]

Thus a whole walk family can be evaluated through three spectral blocks,
rather than expanded into its constituent walks. For example the rational
resolvent is

\[
(I-zA)^{-1}=\frac{P_+}{1-3z}+\frac{P_-}{1+3z}+P_0.
\]

There is also an integral form that directly reads the minimum and its
multiplicity from the complete formal path integral. Put \(R(t)=(I-tA)^{-1}\).
Using only \(A^3=9A\), the checked polynomial identity is

\[
(I-tA)\bigl[(1-9t^2)I+tA+t^2A^2\bigr]=(1-9t^2)I.
\]

Consequently its three endpoint types are

\[
R_{ab}(t)=
\begin{cases}
(1-6t^2)/(1-9t^2),&a=b,\\
3t^2/(1-9t^2),&a\ne b\text{ with the same parity},\\
t/(1-9t^2),&a,b\text{ with opposite parity}.
\end{cases}
\]

Their lowest degrees are respectively \(0,2,1\); their leading coefficients
are \(1,3,1\), exactly the grades and multiplicities of the constructed minimum
words. The script checks this for all 36 endpoint pairs by the polynomial
identity, without generating walk expansions. Since \(1-9t^2\) has constant
coefficient 1, it is a unit in \(\mathbb Z_2[[t]]\): this form is integral and
does not divide by 6. The formal variable \(t\) records path grade; its degree
is distinct from the 2-adic valuation of a coefficient. The explicit word
constructor above supplies the witnesses behind the leading coefficient.

These graph spectral identities are derived and checked here. Their use as a
source proof constructor depends on retaining the source boundary and grading.

**Coefficient domain:** the displayed normalized spectral projectors are over
\(\mathbb Q\) or \(\mathbb C\), and can also be interpreted over
\(\mathbb Q_2\). They are not all integral operators over \(\mathbb Z_2\):
\(1/6\) is not a 2-adic integer. Retain an unnormalized operator and its exact
scale, or explicitly change the coefficient domain. The native odd-denominator
normalizers \(1/3\) and \(1/63\) have a different integrality status.

## 3. What the three recovered papers contribute

**Cohen, Ramanujan Graphs in Polynomial Time [1].** The paper turns a specific
interlacing-family existence argument into deterministic polynomial-time
construction by computing the required conditional expected characteristic
polynomials through a symbolic determinant. A child satisfying the spectral
bound can then be selected repeatedly. The paper explicitly distinguishes this
family from other interlacing families whose required coefficients are #P-hard.
For this programme, the useful template is an efficiently computed conditional
invariant that certifies the selected constructor. Identifying that invariant
for an original target is part of the construction.

**Hall–Puder–Sawin, Ramanujan Coverings of Graphs [2].** Its three-sheeted case
contains exactly the six-frame averaging factorization

\[
\frac{1+s}{2}\;\frac{1+2t}{3}\;\frac{1+s}{2}
=\frac16\sum_{g\in S_3}g,
\]

where \(s=(12)\), \(t=(13)\). Three independent binary choices with respective
reflection probabilities \(1/2,2/3,1/2\) give uniform \(S_3\) mass. The script
checks this identity with exact rational coefficients. This is a genuine local
factorization of the invariant fold; its normalized coefficients still carry
the domain condition above.

**Hsieh–Lee–Mohanty–Putterman–Zhang, Sparsifying Cayley Graphs on Every Group
[3].** Theorem 1.3 gives a relative spectral approximation using
\(O(\log^5|G|/\epsilon^2)\) reweighted generators, for polynomially bounded
weights, with a randomized algorithm polynomial in \(|G|\). Small output does
not by itself give construction polynomial in \(\log|G|\). Its Section 3
also separates Cayley graph sparsification from sparsifying nonabelian group
equations, using \(D_3\cong S_3\) in a lower-bound construction. The operator
to sparsify must therefore be the actual source-linked quadratic form, not an
unverified substitution of graph edges for all source constraints.

## 4. An exact consequence of relative sparsification

For positive semidefinite operators and \(0\le\epsilon<1\),

\[
(1-\epsilon)L\preceq\widetilde L\preceq(1+\epsilon)L
\quad\Longrightarrow\quad
\ker L=\ker\widetilde L.
\]

Proof: the upper inequality sends a zero quadratic form for \(L\) to a zero
quadratic form for \(\widetilde L\); the lower inequality gives the converse.
For a positive semidefinite operator, a zero quadratic form is equivalent to
membership in its kernel.

This is a positive route to exact H24 construction. If the original calibrated
zero-slack fibre is represented faithfully as the zero set of such an energy,
then relative sparsification preserves that fibre exactly. For an affine
constraint \(Bx=b\), retain the augmented residual \([B,-b](x,1)\) and its
quadratic form; dropping \(b\) would change the original judgement. The
\(K_{3,3}\) Laplacian alone expresses equality of the six frame values.

Positive minima need an additional theorem. As an exact example, the diagonal
forms \(L=\operatorname{diag}(0,1,2)\) and
\(\widetilde L=\operatorname{diag}(0,3/2,1)\) obey the above inequalities for
\(\epsilon=1/2\), have identical kernels, but reverse the minimizing choice
between the second and third coordinate vectors. The script checks this
example. Zero-slack construction is consequently a sharper interface than
approximating an arbitrary positive minimum.

## 5. Hadamard, Butson, Golay, and Leech as concrete adapters

A complex Hadamard matrix satisfies \(HH^*=dI\) with unimodular entries;
Butson type restricts entries to roots of unity, so phases can be represented
exactly in a cyclotomic coefficient field [4]. In four ports, the Walsh matrix
\(W_{ij}=(-1)^{\operatorname{popcount}(i\mathbin{\&}j)}\) obeys
\(W^tW=4I\). Its zero-frequency coefficient is exactly the sum of the four
input coefficients, and \(W^{-1}=W/4\). Keeping all four transformed
coefficients retains the inverse; keeping only the sum is an intentional
readout. A spectral transform must also retain the grade/positivity data needed
by a tropical or minimum readout. Unit norm preservation alone does not retain
the location of a minimum.

The supplied Golay/Leech links suggest a possible structured code on six groups
of four coordinates. This becomes an actual adapter once its equations are
given. The script supplies one: multiply a 12-bit polynomial by

\[
g(x)=x^{11}+x^9+x^7+x^6+x^5+x+1
\]

over \(\mathbb F_2\), then append the even-parity bit. Polynomial division
returns the original message. All 4096 words round-trip, and their checked
weight enumerator is
\(1+759y^8+2576y^{12}+759y^{16}+y^{24}\). This verifies a self-dual binary
\([24,12,8]\) code and gives real redundancy/consistency structure. Leech and
Sloane's original construction paper explains the passage from codes to
lattice sphere packings, including the role of the Golay code [5]. An
identification with the programme's 24 coordinates still needs the actual
coordinate map and lattice conditions; the count \(24=6\cdot4\) supplies an
indexing choice, not that identification.

## 6. Mellin, Laplace, and z evaluations of the same family

The standard Mellin transform has an inversion theorem and Parseval identities
under specified integrability conditions [6]. Direct substitution \(x=e^u\)
gives

\[
\mathcal Mf(c+i\xi)=\int_{\mathbb R}
  e^{cu}f(e^u)e^{i\xi u}\,du.
\]

This is Fourier evaluation on logarithmic scale. In the corresponding
\(L^2\) domain, its norm is

\[
\int_0^\infty |f(x)|^2x^{2c-1}\,dx
=\frac1{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb R}|\mathcal Mf(c+i\xi)|^2\,d\xi.
\]

For a path-grade coefficient family \(a_n\), define
\(F(z)=\sum a_nz^n\). The changes of variable
\(Z(w)=F(w^{-1})\) and
\(\mathcal L(\sum a_n\delta_{nT})(s)=F(e^{-sT})=Z(e^{sT})\)
express the same family in ordinary generating, z, and Laplace coordinates,
where the respective sums/convergence domains exist. Fractional sampling adds
the marked phase \(\theta\), giving
\(Z_\theta(w)=\sum f((n+\theta)T)w^{-n}\). This specifies what an advanced
z adapter must retain; it does not infer unsupplied intersample data.

The computational opportunity is to factor or solve the operator in the
coordinate system where it is simple, then return through a proved inverse.
The necessary constructor remains the one that inhabits the original marked
minimum fibre. The graph construction above is one fully explicit instance.
