Concrete one- and two-coordinate source generator: full Lean proof source

Focused_Original_Proofs/Hilbert24_Construct_Minimum.lean · SHA-256 7ea9b0a027caeb0a5be29e6317be82cbe97316a2aaf6f5e070d91317eec61469

import Hilbert24_Calibrated_Policy
import Hilbert24_SAT_Return

/-
Direct source instantiation of the EXISTING Hilbert24Policy.constructMinimum.
The policy selects structural one-child source contractions; only its terminal
rule observes the closed source family. There is no supplied SAT bit, source
certificate, minimum candidate, precomputed counting circuit, or policy oracle.

The calibration potential is a source-indexed definition occurring in proofs.
It is not evaluated first to choose the constructor. Rule charges count the
original candidate's code grade. Acquiring/evaluating a coefficient family has
its own real cost, for which no uniform polynomial bound is asserted here.
-/

namespace Hilbert24Construct
open Hilbert24Policy Hilbert24SAT

def one {n : Nat} (V : Bits (n+1) → Bool) : Bits n → Bool :=
  fun a => V (false,a) || V (true,a)

def two {n : Nat} (V : Bits (n+2) → Bool) : Bits n → Bool :=
  fun a => quartetElimination
    (V (false,(false,a))) (V (false,(true,a)))
    (V (true,(false,a))) (V (true,(true,a)))

/-- Closed support observation, defined on the same dependent source family.
    This definition is used by the calibration theorem, not as an input oracle. -/
def sourceTag : (n : Nat) → (Bits n → Bool) → Bool
  | 0, V => V ()
  | n+1, V => sourceTag n (one V)

theorem sourceTag_pointwise (n : Nat) (V W : Bits n → Bool)
    (same : ∀ a, V a = W a) : sourceTag n V = sourceTag n W := by
  induction n with
  | zero => exact same ()
  | succ n ih =>
      apply ih
      intro a
      change (V (false,a) || V (true,a)) = (W (false,a) || W (true,a))
      rw [same, same]

theorem four_reorders (a b c d : Bool) :
    (a || c || (b || d)) = quartetElimination a b c d := by
  cases a <;> cases b <;> cases c <;> cases d <;> rfl

theorem sourceTag_two (n : Nat) (V : Bits (n+2) → Bool) :
    sourceTag (n+2) V = sourceTag n (two V) := by
  change sourceTag n (one (one V)) = sourceTag n (two V)
  apply sourceTag_pointwise
  intro a
  exact four_reorders _ _ _ _

theorem sourceTag_true (n : Nat) (V : Bits n → Bool) :
    sourceTag n V = true ↔ ∃ a, V a = true := by
  induction n with
  | zero =>
      constructor
      · intro h; exact ⟨(),h⟩
      · intro h; obtain ⟨a,ha⟩ := h; cases a; exact ha
  | succ n ih =>
      change sourceTag n (one V) = true ↔ _
      constructor
      · intro h
        obtain ⟨a,ha⟩ := (ih (one V)).mp h
        cases h0 : V (false,a) with
        | true => exact ⟨(false,a),h0⟩
        | false =>
            cases h1 : V (true,a) with
            | true => exact ⟨(true,a),h1⟩
            | false =>
                change (V (false,a) || V (true,a)) = true at ha
                rw [h0,h1] at ha
                cases ha
      · intro h
        obtain ⟨⟨b,a⟩,ha⟩ := h
        apply (ih (one V)).mpr
        refine ⟨a,?_⟩
        change (V (false,a) || V (true,a)) = true
        cases b with
        | false => rw [ha]; rfl
        | true => rw [ha]; exact Bool.or_true _

theorem sourceTag_false (n : Nat) (V : Bits n → Bool) :
    sourceTag n V = false ↔ ∀ a, V a = false := by
  constructor
  · intro h a
    cases ha : V a with
    | false => rfl
    | true =>
        have ht := (sourceTag_true n V).mpr ⟨a,ha⟩
        rw [h] at ht
        cases ht
  · intro h
    cases ht : sourceTag n V with
    | false => rfl
    | true =>
        obtain ⟨a,ha⟩ := (sourceTag_true n V).mp ht
        rw [h a] at ha
        cases ha

def chosenOne {n : Nat} (V : Bits (n+1) → Bool) (a : Bits n) : Bits (n+1) :=
  ((!V (false,a)),a)

theorem chosenOne_exact {n : Nat} (V : Bits (n+1) → Bool) (a : Bits n) :
    V (chosenOne V a) = one V a := by
  unfold chosenOne one
  cases h0 : V (false,a) with
  | false => rfl
  | true => exact h0

def chosenTwo {n : Nat} (V : Bits (n+2) → Bool) (a : Bits n) : Bits (n+2) :=
  let b := selectQuartet (V (false,(false,a))) (V (false,(true,a)))
    (V (true,(false,a))) (V (true,(true,a)))
  (b.fst,(b.snd,a))

theorem read_original_two {n : Nat} (V : Bits (n+2) → Bool)
    (x y : Bool) (a : Bits n) :
    readQuartet x y (V (false,(false,a))) (V (false,(true,a)))
      (V (true,(false,a))) (V (true,(true,a))) = V (x,(y,a)) := by
  cases x <;> cases y <;> rfl

theorem chosenTwo_exact {n : Nat} (V : Bits (n+2) → Bool) (a : Bits n) :
    V (chosenTwo V a) = two V a := by
  let b := selectQuartet (V (false,(false,a))) (V (false,(true,a)))
    (V (true,(false,a))) (V (true,(true,a)))
  change V (b.fst,(b.snd,a)) = two V a
  exact (read_original_two V b.fst b.snd a).symm.trans
    (quartet_selector_exact _ _ _ _)

def liftOne {n : Nat} (V : Bits (n+1) → Bool) : Candidate (one V) → Candidate V
  | .fallback => .fallback
  | .satisfying a h => .satisfying (chosenOne V a) ((chosenOne_exact V a).trans h)

def liftTwo {n : Nat} (V : Bits (n+2) → Bool) : Candidate (two V) → Candidate V
  | .fallback => .fallback
  | .satisfying a h => .satisfying (chosenTwo V a) ((chosenTwo_exact V a).trans h)

theorem liftOne_grade {n : Nat} (V : Bits (n+1) → Bool) (p : Candidate (one V)) :
    grade (n+1) (liftOne V p) = 1 + grade n p := by
  cases p with
  | fallback =>
      change (n+1)+2=1+(n+2)
      exact (Nat.add_assoc n 1 2).trans (Nat.add_left_comm n 1 2)
  | satisfying a h =>
      change (n+1)+1=1+(n+1)
      exact (Nat.add_assoc n 1 1).trans (Nat.add_left_comm n 1 1)

theorem liftTwo_grade {n : Nat} (V : Bits (n+2) → Bool) (p : Candidate (two V)) :
    grade (n+2) (liftTwo V p) = 2 + grade n p := by
  cases p with
  | fallback =>
      change (n+2)+2=2+(n+2)
      exact (Nat.add_assoc n 2 2).trans (Nat.add_left_comm n 2 2)
  | satisfying a h =>
      change (n+2)+1=2+(n+1)
      exact (Nat.add_assoc n 2 1).trans (Nat.add_left_comm n 2 1)

structure Goal where
  width : Nat
  verifier : Bits width → Bool

inductive SourceRule
  | splitOne (n : Nat) (V : Bits (n+1) → Bool)
  | splitTwo (n : Nat) (V : Bits (n+2) → Bool)
  | model (n : Nat) (V : Bits n → Bool) (a : Bits n) (valid : V a = true)
  | fallback (n : Nat) (V : Bits n → Bool)

def conclusion : SourceRule → Goal
  | .splitOne n V => ⟨n+1,V⟩
  | .splitTwo n V => ⟨n+2,V⟩
  | .model n V _ _ => ⟨n,V⟩
  | .fallback n V => ⟨n,V⟩

def arity : SourceRule → Nat
  | .splitOne _ _ => 1
  | .splitTwo _ _ => 1
  | .model _ _ _ _ => 0
  | .fallback _ _ => 0

def premise : (r : SourceRule) → Fin (arity r) → Goal
  | .splitOne n V, _ => ⟨n,one V⟩
  | .splitTwo n V, _ => ⟨n,two V⟩
  | .model _ _ _ _, i => Fin.elim0 i
  | .fallback _ _, i => Fin.elim0 i

def charge : SourceRule → Nat
  | .splitOne _ _ => 1
  | .splitTwo _ _ => 2
  | .model n _ _ _ => n+1
  | .fallback n _ => n+2

def sourceSystem : RuleSystem Goal :=
  ⟨SourceRule,conclusion,arity,premise,charge⟩

def potential (q : Goal) : Nat :=
  q.width + if sourceTag q.width q.verifier then 1 else 2

def slack : SourceRule → Nat
  | .splitOne _ _ => 0
  | .splitTwo _ _ => 0
  | .model _ _ _ _ => 0
  | .fallback n V => if sourceTag n V then 1 else 0

theorem local_calibration (r : SourceRule) :
    charge r + sumFin (fun i => potential (premise r i)) =
      potential (conclusion r) + slack r := by
  cases r with
  | splitOne n V =>
      change 1 + ((n + if sourceTag n (one V) then 1 else 2)+0) =
        ((n+1) + if sourceTag n (one V) then 1 else 2)+0
      rw [Nat.add_zero,Nat.add_zero,Nat.add_comm n 1,Nat.add_assoc]
  | splitTwo n V =>
      change 2 + ((n + if sourceTag n (two V) then 1 else 2)+0) =
        ((n+2) + if sourceTag (n+2) V then 1 else 2)+0
      rw [sourceTag_two,Nat.add_zero,Nat.add_zero,Nat.add_comm n 2,Nat.add_assoc]
  | model n V a valid =>
      have h := (sourceTag_true n V).mpr ⟨a,valid⟩
      change (n+1)+0=(n + if sourceTag n V then 1 else 2)+0
      rw [h]
      rfl
  | fallback n V =>
      change (n+2)+0 = (n + if sourceTag n V then 1 else 2) +
        (if sourceTag n V then 1 else 0)
      cases h : sourceTag n V <;> rfl

def sourceCalibration : Calibration sourceSystem where
  potential := potential
  slack := slack
  local_identity := local_calibration

def chosenBase (V : Bits 0 → Bool) : {r : SourceRule // conclusion r = ⟨0,V⟩} :=
  if h : V () = true then ⟨.model 0 V () h,rfl⟩ else ⟨.fallback 0 V,rfl⟩

theorem chosenBase_tight (V : Bits 0 → Bool) : slack (chosenBase V).val = 0 := by
  unfold chosenBase
  split
  · rfl
  · rename_i h
    change (if V () = true then 1 else 0) = 0
    rw [if_neg h]

theorem chosenBase_arity (V : Bits 0 → Bool) : arity (chosenBase V).val = 0 := by
  unfold chosenBase
  split <;> rfl

/-- Structural choice. The only data-dependent rule choice is V() at width 0.
    This function neither calls sourceTag at positive width nor accepts a SAT bit. -/
def chosen : (q : Goal) → {r : SourceRule // conclusion r = q}
  | ⟨0,V⟩ => chosenBase V
  | ⟨1,V⟩ => ⟨.splitOne 0 V,rfl⟩
  | ⟨n+2,V⟩ => ⟨.splitTwo n V,rfl⟩

theorem chosen_tight (q : Goal) : slack (chosen q).val = 0 := by
  cases q with
  | mk n V =>
      cases n with
      | zero => exact chosenBase_tight V
      | succ n => cases n <;> rfl

theorem chosen_decrease (q : Goal) (i : Fin (arity (chosen q).val)) :
    (premise (chosen q).val i).width < q.width := by
  cases q with
  | mk n V =>
      cases n with
      | zero =>
          change Fin (arity (chosenBase V).val) at i
          rw [chosenBase_arity] at i
          exact Fin.elim0 i
      | succ n =>
          cases n with
          | zero => exact Nat.zero_lt_succ 0
          | succ n => exact Nat.lt_trans (Nat.lt_succ_self n) (Nat.lt_succ_self (n+1))

def sourcePolicy : TightPolicy sourceSystem sourceCalibration where
  chosen := chosen
  tight := chosen_tight
  rank := Goal.width
  decrease := chosen_decrease

def meaning (q : Goal) : Type := Candidate q.verifier

def originalConstructor (r : SourceRule)
    (children : (i : Fin (arity r)) → meaning (premise r i)) : meaning (conclusion r) :=
  match r with
  | .splitOne _ V => liftOne V (children ⟨0,Nat.zero_lt_succ 0⟩)
  | .splitTwo _ V => liftTwo V (children ⟨0,Nat.zero_lt_succ 0⟩)
  | .model _ _ a h => .satisfying a h
  | .fallback _ _ => .fallback

def decode {q : Goal} (p : Derivation sourceSystem q) : Candidate q.verifier :=
  interpret meaning originalConstructor p

theorem decode_grade {q : Goal} (p : Derivation sourceSystem q) :
    grade q.width (decode p) = work p := by
  induction p with
  | node r children ih =>
      cases r with
      | splitOne n V =>
          change grade (n+1) (liftOne V (decode (children ⟨0,Nat.zero_lt_succ 0⟩))) = _
          have ih0 : grade n (decode (children ⟨0,Nat.zero_lt_succ 0⟩)) =
              work (children ⟨0,Nat.zero_lt_succ 0⟩) := ih _
          rw [liftOne_grade,ih0]
          rfl
      | splitTwo n V =>
          change grade (n+2) (liftTwo V (decode (children ⟨0,Nat.zero_lt_succ 0⟩))) = _
          have ih0 : grade n (decode (children ⟨0,Nat.zero_lt_succ 0⟩)) =
              work (children ⟨0,Nat.zero_lt_succ 0⟩) := ih _
          rw [liftTwo_grade,ih0]
          rfl
      | model n V a h => rfl
      | fallback n V => rfl

def embed {n : Nat} {V : Bits n → Bool} (p : Candidate V) : Derivation sourceSystem ⟨n,V⟩ :=
  match p with
  | .fallback => Derivation.node (S := sourceSystem) (SourceRule.fallback n V) (fun i => Fin.elim0 i)
  | .satisfying a h => Derivation.node (S := sourceSystem) (SourceRule.model n V a h) (fun i => Fin.elim0 i)

theorem embed_work {n : Nat} {V : Bits n → Bool} (p : Candidate V) :
    work (embed p) = grade n p := by
  cases p <;> rfl

theorem decode_embed {n : Nat} {V : Bits n → Bool} (p : Candidate V) :
    decode (embed p) = p := by
  cases p <;> rfl

/-- THE EXECUTION ENTRY POINT. Calls the existing calibrated constructor
    immediately, using the explicit structural policy above. -/
def constructMinimum (n : Nat) (V : Bits n → Bool) :
    Optimal (grade (verify := V) n) :=
  let native := Hilbert24Policy.constructMinimum sourcePolicy ⟨n,V⟩
  { witness := decode native.derivation
    least := fun competitor => by
      calc
        grade n (decode native.derivation) = work native.derivation := decode_grade native.derivation
        _ ≤ work (embed competitor) := native.optimal (embed competitor)
        _ = grade n competitor := embed_work competitor }

/-- The actual existing constructor unfolds to one child holding the complete
    two-coordinate family, followed by the exact original-source lift. -/
theorem constructed_two (n : Nat) (V : Bits (n+2) → Bool) :
    (constructMinimum (n+2) V).witness =
      liftTwo V (constructMinimum n (two V)).witness := by
  change decode (Hilbert24Policy.build sourcePolicy ⟨n+2,V⟩) = _
  rw [Hilbert24Policy.build]
  rfl

/-- Odd input widths use a genuine one-coordinate final contraction. -/
theorem constructed_one (V : Bits 1 → Bool) :
    (constructMinimum 1 V).witness =
      liftOne V (constructMinimum 0 (one V)).witness := by
  change decode (Hilbert24Policy.build sourcePolicy ⟨1,V⟩) = _
  rw [Hilbert24Policy.build]
  rfl

theorem constructed_original_minimum (n : Nat) (V : Bits n → Bool)
    (competitor : Candidate V) :
    grade n (constructMinimum n V).witness ≤ grade n competitor :=
  (constructMinimum n V).least competitor

theorem constructed_exact_grade (n : Nat) (V : Bits n → Bool) :
    grade n (constructMinimum n V).witness = potential ⟨n,V⟩ := by
  let native := Hilbert24Policy.constructMinimum sourcePolicy ⟨n,V⟩
  change grade n (decode native.derivation) = potential ⟨n,V⟩
  exact (decode_grade native.derivation).trans native.exact_cost

def originalCertificate (n : Nat) (V : Bits n → Bool) : Certificate V :=
  certificateFromMinimum n (constructMinimum n V)

theorem original_certificate_bit (n : Nat) (V : Bits n → Bool) :
    answerBit (originalCertificate n V) = sourceTag n V := by
  cases h : sourceTag n V with
  | true =>
      exact (answerBit_true_iff _).mpr ((sourceTag_true n V).mp h)
  | false =>
      exact (answerBit_false_iff _).mpr ((sourceTag_false n V).mp h)

def chosenGrade (n : Nat) (V : Bits n → Bool) : Nat :=
  grade n (constructMinimum n V).witness

def sourceBits : (n : Nat) → Bits n → List Bool
  | 0, _ => []
  | n+1, a => a.fst :: sourceBits n a.snd

def returnedBits (n : Nat) (V : Bits n → Bool) : Option (List Bool) :=
  match (constructMinimum n V).witness with
  | .fallback => none
  | .satisfying a _ => some (sourceBits n a)

def example3 (a : Bits 3) : Bool :=
  (a.fst || a.snd.fst || a.snd.snd.fst) &&
  ((!a.fst) || (!a.snd.fst) || a.snd.snd.fst)

def impossible3 (a : Bits 3) : Bool := example3 a && a.snd.snd.fst && !a.snd.snd.fst
def exactlyOne (a : Bits 1) : Bool := a.fst
def impossibleOne (a : Bits 1) : Bool := a.fst && !a.fst

#eval chosenGrade 0 (fun _ => true)
#eval chosenGrade 0 (fun _ => false)
#eval chosenGrade 1 exactlyOne
#eval chosenGrade 1 impossibleOne
#eval returnedBits 1 exactlyOne
#eval chosenGrade 3 example3
#eval chosenGrade 3 impossible3
#eval returnedBits 3 example3
#eval answerBit (originalCertificate 3 impossible3)

end Hilbert24Construct
Original certificate return, candidate grade, and separate operational reserve: full Lean source

Focused_Original_Proofs/Hilbert24_SAT_Return.lean · SHA-256 cb3074f4dd8eeaef09506bd9f23d8de3d88a64f3c94dffd65229a52773d1db40

/-
Constructive H24/SAT interface: exact source certificates, minima, native
returns, Boolean elimination, and a separately charged computation bound.

The objective grade encodes the SAT decision. It is NOT asserted to equal
the work needed to discover a minimizer. The reserve theorem charges that
work separately. No uniform all-CNF polynomial producer is assumed solved.
-/

namespace Hilbert24SAT

universe u v w

inductive Certificate {A : Type u} (verify : A → Bool) : Type u
  | sat (assignment : A) (verified : verify assignment = true)
  | unsat (all_false : ∀ assignment : A, verify assignment = false)

inductive Candidate {A : Type u} (verify : A → Bool) : Type u
  | fallback
  | satisfying (assignment : A) (verified : verify assignment = true)

/-- An inhabited objective family for either answer.  Fallback by itself is
    only a candidate; its OPTIMALITY is what supplies the UNSAT certificate. -/
def grade {A : Type u} {verify : A → Bool} (n : Nat) : Candidate verify → Nat
  | .fallback => n + 2
  | .satisfying _ _ => n + 1

structure Optimal {P : Type u} (cost : P → Nat) where
  witness : P
  least : ∀ competitor : P, cost witness ≤ cost competitor

def certificateFromMinimum {A : Type u} {verify : A → Bool} (n : Nat)
    (minimum : Optimal (grade (verify := verify) n)) : Certificate verify := by
  cases minimum with
  | mk candidate least =>
      cases candidate with
      | satisfying assignment verified => exact .sat assignment verified
      | fallback =>
          apply Certificate.unsat
          intro assignment
          cases h : verify assignment with
          | false => rfl
          | true =>
              have impossible : n + 2 ≤ n + 1 := least (.satisfying assignment h)
              exact False.elim ((Nat.not_le_of_gt (Nat.lt_succ_self (n + 1))) impossible)

def minimumFromCertificate {A : Type u} {verify : A → Bool} (n : Nat)
    (certificate : Certificate verify) : Optimal (grade (verify := verify) n) := by
  cases certificate with
  | sat assignment verified =>
      refine ⟨.satisfying assignment verified, ?_⟩
      intro competitor
      cases competitor with
      | fallback => exact Nat.le_succ (n + 1)
      | satisfying _ _ => exact Nat.le_refl (n + 1)
  | unsat all_false =>
      refine ⟨.fallback, ?_⟩
      intro competitor
      cases competitor with
      | fallback => exact Nat.le_refl (n + 2)
      | satisfying assignment verified =>
          have impossible : false = true := (all_false assignment).symm.trans verified
          cases impossible

theorem certificate_roundtrip {A : Type u} {verify : A → Bool} (n : Nat)
    (certificate : Certificate verify) :
    certificateFromMinimum n (minimumFromCertificate n certificate) = certificate := by
  cases certificate <;> rfl

theorem minimum_roundtrip {A : Type u} {verify : A → Bool} (n : Nat)
    (minimum : Optimal (grade (verify := verify) n)) :
    minimumFromCertificate n (certificateFromMinimum n minimum) = minimum := by
  cases minimum with
  | mk candidate least => cases candidate <;> rfl

/-- The full objective always has at least its fallback candidate. -/
def fallbackCandidate {A : Type u} (verify : A → Bool) : Candidate verify := .fallback

/-- A genuine source one-variable elimination. -/
def originalBit (x r p n : Bool) : Bool :=
  r && (x || p) && ((!x) || n)

def eliminatedBit (r p n : Bool) : Bool := r && (p || n)

theorem elimination_exact (r p n : Bool) :
    (originalBit false r p n || originalBit true r p n) = eliminatedBit r p n := by
  cases r <;> cases p <;> cases n <;> rfl

theorem selector_exact (r p n : Bool) :
    originalBit (!p) r p n = eliminatedBit r p n := by
  cases r <;> cases p <;> cases n <;> rfl

theorem original_false_of_eliminated_false (x r p n : Bool)
    (h : eliminatedBit r p n = false) : originalBit x r p n = false := by
  cases x <;> cases r <;> cases p <;> cases n <;> first | rfl | exact h

/-- Both answer branches return an ORIGINAL assignment or ORIGINAL universal
    refutation after Boolean elimination; the selector is x=not P(a). -/
def liftElimination {A : Type u} (R P N : A → Bool)
    (certificate : Certificate (fun a => eliminatedBit (R a) (P a) (N a))) :
    Certificate (fun xa : Bool × A => originalBit xa.fst (R xa.snd) (P xa.snd) (N xa.snd)) := by
  cases certificate with
  | sat a verified =>
      apply Certificate.sat (!P a, a)
      change originalBit (!P a) (R a) (P a) (N a) = true
      rw [selector_exact]
      exact verified
  | unsat all_false =>
      apply Certificate.unsat
      intro xa
      exact original_false_of_eliminated_false xa.fst (R xa.snd) (P xa.snd) (N xa.snd)
        (all_false xa.snd)

/- Four exact cofactor ports, ordered 00, 01, 10, 11. These are the semantic
   return conditions for a packed two-bit block; no CRT packing algorithm or
   machine-cost bound is silently inferred from these Boolean identities. -/
def quartetElimination (b00 b01 b10 b11 : Bool) : Bool :=
  b00 || b01 || b10 || b11

def readQuartet (x y b00 b01 b10 b11 : Bool) : Bool :=
  match x, y with
  | false, false => b00
  | false, true => b01
  | true, false => b10
  | true, true => b11

/-- First true port in the fixed order. If every port is false the selected
    pair is 11, and its cofactor is false, preserving the exact equation. -/
def selectQuartet (b00 b01 b10 _b11 : Bool) : Bool × Bool :=
  if b00 then (false, false)
  else if b01 then (false, true)
  else if b10 then (true, false)
  else (true, true)

theorem quartet_selector_exact (b00 b01 b10 b11 : Bool) :
    readQuartet (selectQuartet b00 b01 b10 b11).fst
      (selectQuartet b00 b01 b10 b11).snd b00 b01 b10 b11 =
        quartetElimination b00 b01 b10 b11 := by
  cases b00 <;> cases b01 <;> cases b10 <;> cases b11 <;> rfl

theorem quartet_selector_returns_true (b00 b01 b10 b11 : Bool)
    (h : quartetElimination b00 b01 b10 b11 = true) :
    readQuartet (selectQuartet b00 b01 b10 b11).fst
      (selectQuartet b00 b01 b10 b11).snd b00 b01 b10 b11 = true := by
  rw [quartet_selector_exact]
  exact h

theorem quartet_false_returns_all_four (b00 b01 b10 b11 : Bool)
    (h : quartetElimination b00 b01 b10 b11 = false) :
    b00 = false ∧ b01 = false ∧ b10 = false ∧ b11 = false := by
  cases b00 <;> cases b01 <;> cases b10 <;> cases b11 <;>
    first | exact ⟨rfl, rfl, rfl, rfl⟩ | cases h

theorem quartet_false_returns_every_pair (x y b00 b01 b10 b11 : Bool)
    (h : quartetElimination b00 b01 b10 b11 = false) :
    readQuartet x y b00 b01 b10 b11 = false := by
  obtain ⟨h00, h01, h10, h11⟩ := quartet_false_returns_all_four b00 b01 b10 b11 h
  cases x with
  | false => cases y with
    | false => exact h00
    | true => exact h01
  | true => cases y with
    | false => exact h10
    | true => exact h11

/-- Uniform original return over ANY residual-assignment type. The SAT branch
    returns the selected original bit pair together with the residual witness;
    the UNSAT branch returns falsity for every original pair and residual. -/
def liftTwoBitElimination {A : Type u} (F00 F01 F10 F11 : A → Bool)
    (certificate : Certificate (fun a => quartetElimination (F00 a) (F01 a) (F10 a) (F11 a))) :
    Certificate (fun xya : (Bool × Bool) × A =>
      readQuartet xya.fst.fst xya.fst.snd
        (F00 xya.snd) (F01 xya.snd) (F10 xya.snd) (F11 xya.snd)) := by
  cases certificate with
  | sat a verified =>
      apply Certificate.sat (selectQuartet (F00 a) (F01 a) (F10 a) (F11 a), a)
      exact quartet_selector_returns_true (F00 a) (F01 a) (F10 a) (F11 a) verified
  | unsat all_false =>
      apply Certificate.unsat
      intro xya
      exact quartet_false_returns_every_pair xya.fst.fst xya.fst.snd
        (F00 xya.snd) (F01 xya.snd) (F10 xya.snd) (F11 xya.snd) (all_false xya.snd)

/- A complete executable finite Boolean certificate producer. Its n is the
   original number of bits, not an artificial proof-depth limit. The method
   can evaluate 2^n leaves; no polynomial work claim is made for this solver. -/
def Bits : Nat → Type
  | 0 => Unit
  | n + 1 => Bool × Bits n

def solveFinite : (n : Nat) → (verify : Bits n → Bool) → Certificate verify
  | 0, verify =>
      match h : verify () with
      | true => .sat () h
      | false => .unsat (fun a => by cases a; exact h)
  | n + 1, verify =>
      match solveFinite n (fun a => verify (false, a)) with
      | .sat a verified => .sat (false, a) verified
      | .unsat no_false =>
          match solveFinite n (fun a => verify (true, a)) with
          | .sat a verified => .sat (true, a) verified
          | .unsat no_true =>
              .unsat (fun xa => by
                cases xa with
                | mk x a => cases x with
                  | false => exact no_false a
                  | true => exact no_true a)

def finiteH24 (n : Nat) (verify : Bits n → Bool) : Optimal (grade (verify := verify) n) :=
  minimumFromCertificate n (solveFinite n verify)

/- Native is the EXACT typed image, not every ambient torser matrix. -/
structure NativeChart {A : Type u} (verify : A → Bool) (Native : Type v) where
  encode : Candidate verify → Native
  decode : Native → Candidate verify
  decode_encode : ∀ p, decode (encode p) = p
  encode_decode : ∀ m, encode (decode m) = m

def nativeGrade {A : Type u} {verify : A → Bool} {Native : Type v}
    (chart : NativeChart verify Native) (n : Nat) (m : Native) : Nat :=
  grade n (chart.decode m)

theorem native_grade_return {A : Type u} {verify : A → Bool} {Native : Type v}
    (chart : NativeChart verify Native) (n : Nat) (p : Candidate verify) :
    nativeGrade chart n (chart.encode p) = grade n p := by
  change grade n (chart.decode (chart.encode p)) = grade n p
  rw [chart.decode_encode]

def sourceMinimumFromNative {A : Type u} {verify : A → Bool} {Native : Type v}
    (chart : NativeChart verify Native) (n : Nat)
    (minimum : Optimal (nativeGrade chart n)) : Optimal (grade (verify := verify) n) := by
  refine ⟨chart.decode minimum.witness, ?_⟩
  intro competitor
  calc
    grade n (chart.decode minimum.witness) ≤ nativeGrade chart n (chart.encode competitor) :=
      minimum.least (chart.encode competitor)
    _ = grade n competitor := native_grade_return chart n competitor

def certificateFromNativeMinimum {A : Type u} {verify : A → Bool} {Native : Type v}
    (chart : NativeChart verify Native) (n : Nat)
    (minimum : Optimal (nativeGrade chart n)) : Certificate verify :=
  certificateFromMinimum n (sourceMinimumFromNative chart n minimum)

/- The following work is OPERATIONAL CHARGE, separate from the two-valued
   objective grade above. Step must be the intended fully charged machine
   semantics, including representation operations, verification, and return. -/
inductive Trace {State : Type u} (Step : State → State → Nat → Prop) :
    State → State → Type u
  | done (s : State) : Trace Step s s
  | next {s t z : State} (charge : Nat) (step : Step s t charge)
      (rest : Trace Step t z) : Trace Step s z

def work {State : Type u} {Step : State → State → Nat → Prop} :
    {s z : State} → Trace Step s z → Nat
  | _, _, .done _ => 0
  | _, _, .next charge _ rest => charge + work rest

theorem reserve_telescopes {State : Type u} {Step : State → State → Nat → Prop}
    (reserve : State → Nat)
    (local_charge : ∀ s t charge, Step s t charge → charge + reserve t ≤ reserve s)
    {s z : State} (trace : Trace Step s z) :
    work trace + reserve z ≤ reserve s := by
  induction trace with
  | done s =>
      change 0 + reserve s ≤ reserve s
      rw [Nat.zero_add]
      exact Nat.le_refl _
  | @next s t z charge step rest ih =>
      change (charge + work rest) + reserve z ≤ reserve s
      rw [Nat.add_assoc]
      exact Nat.le_trans (Nat.add_le_add_left ih charge) (local_charge s t charge step)

theorem work_le_initial_reserve {State : Type u} {Step : State → State → Nat → Prop}
    (reserve : State → Nat)
    (local_charge : ∀ s t charge, Step s t charge → charge + reserve t ≤ reserve s)
    {s z : State} (trace : Trace Step s z) : work trace ≤ reserve s :=
  Nat.le_trans (Nat.le_add_right _ _) (reserve_telescopes reserve local_charge trace)

def polynomialBudget (coefficient degree offset inputSize : Nat) : Nat :=
  coefficient * (inputSize + 1)^degree + offset

/-- Explicit source-size theorem. The initial reserve estimate is an INPUT;
    it is not derived merely from proof minimality or coefficient precision. -/
theorem source_polynomial_work_bound {Input : Type u} {State : Type v}
    {Step : State → State → Nat → Prop}
    (size : Input → Nat) (start : Input → State) (reserve : State → Nat)
    (coefficient degree offset : Nat)
    (local_charge : ∀ s t charge, Step s t charge → charge + reserve t ≤ reserve s)
    (initial_budget : ∀ input, reserve (start input) ≤
      polynomialBudget coefficient degree offset (size input))
    (input : Input) {finish : State} (trace : Trace Step (start input) finish) :
    work trace ≤ polynomialBudget coefficient degree offset (size input) :=
  Nat.le_trans (work_le_initial_reserve reserve local_charge trace) (initial_budget input)

/-- A completed producer must supply its finite trace AND an original answer
    at its terminal state. The reserve theorem alone does not prove completion. -/
structure CompletedProduction {A : Type u} (verify : A → Bool)
    {State : Type v} (Step : State → State → Nat → Prop) (start : State)
    (TerminalAnswer : State → Certificate verify → Prop) where
  finish : State
  trace : Trace Step start finish
  answer : Certificate verify
  terminal_return : TerminalAnswer finish answer

def answerBit {A : Type u} {verify : A → Bool} : Certificate verify → Bool
  | .sat _ _ => true
  | .unsat _ => false

theorem answerBit_true_iff {A : Type u} {verify : A → Bool}
    (certificate : Certificate verify) :
    answerBit certificate = true ↔ ∃ assignment, verify assignment = true := by
  cases certificate with
  | sat assignment verified =>
      constructor
      · intro _; exact ⟨assignment, verified⟩
      · intro _; rfl
  | unsat all_false =>
      constructor
      · intro impossible; cases impossible
      · intro exists_assignment
        obtain ⟨assignment, verified⟩ := exists_assignment
        have impossible : false = true := (all_false assignment).symm.trans verified
        cases impossible

theorem answerBit_false_iff {A : Type u} {verify : A → Bool}
    (certificate : Certificate verify) :
    answerBit certificate = false ↔ ∀ assignment, verify assignment = false := by
  cases certificate with
  | sat assignment verified =>
      constructor
      · intro impossible; cases impossible
      · intro all_false
        have impossible : false = true := (all_false assignment).symm.trans verified
        cases impossible
  | unsat all_false =>
      constructor
      · intro _; exact all_false
      · intro _; rfl

theorem minimum_decides_original_sat {A : Type u} {verify : A → Bool} (n : Nat)
    (minimum : Optimal (grade (verify := verify) n)) :
    answerBit (certificateFromMinimum n minimum) = true ↔
      ∃ assignment, verify assignment = true :=
  answerBit_true_iff _

theorem native_minimum_decides_original_sat {A : Type u} {verify : A → Bool}
    {Native : Type v} (chart : NativeChart verify Native) (n : Nat)
    (minimum : Optimal (nativeGrade chart n)) :
    answerBit (certificateFromNativeMinimum chart n minimum) = true ↔
      ∃ assignment, verify assignment = true :=
  answerBit_true_iff _

/- A concrete reserve trace checks the arithmetic model itself. It is not
   presented as the cost model of solveFinite or of a CNF algorithm. -/
def creditStep (before after charge : Nat) : Prop := before = charge + after

def creditTrace : Trace creditStep 7 0 :=
  .next (t := 5) 2 rfl (.next (t := 2) 3 rfl (.next (t := 0) 2 rfl (.done 0)))

theorem credit_local (s t charge : Nat) (step : creditStep s t charge) :
    charge + t ≤ s := by
  change s = charge + t at step
  rw [step]
  exact Nat.le_refl _

theorem credit_trace_bound : work creditTrace ≤ 7 :=
  work_le_initial_reserve (fun n => n) credit_local creditTrace

def twoBitVerifier (assignment : Bits 2) : Bool := assignment.fst && !assignment.snd.fst
def impossibleVerifier (assignment : Bits 2) : Bool := assignment.fst && !assignment.fst

#eval answerBit (solveFinite 2 twoBitVerifier)
#eval answerBit (solveFinite 2 impossibleVerifier)
#eval grade 2 (finiteH24 2 twoBitVerifier).witness
#eval grade 2 (finiteH24 2 impossibleVerifier).witness
#eval work creditTrace
#eval selectQuartet false true true false
#eval selectQuartet false false true true

#print axioms minimumFromCertificate
#print axioms certificate_roundtrip
#print axioms minimum_roundtrip

end Hilbert24SAT
Exact original-CNF adapter: full Lean proof source

Focused_Original_Proofs/Hilbert24_CNF_Construct.lean · SHA-256 fde07ecbe972302aa6d01ebbc0e9448e441f3413d6b01608e8e53f3fb0ef2911

import Hilbert24_Construct_Minimum

/-
Transparent original-CNF entry point. Clauses and their variable marks are
retained by the verifier passed directly into the constructed source policy.
This is a semantic source adapter; the Python coefficient compiler is tested
and certificate-replayed separately, not silently identified with this code.
-/

namespace Hilbert24CNF
open Hilbert24SAT Hilbert24Construct

structure Literal (n : Nat) where
  index : Fin n
  positive : Bool

abbrev CNF (n : Nat) := List (List (Literal n))

def readBit : (n : Nat) → Bits n → Fin n → Bool
  | 0, _, i => Fin.elim0 i
  | _+1, a, ⟨0,_⟩ => a.fst
  | n+1, a, ⟨k+1,h⟩ => readBit n a.snd ⟨k,Nat.lt_of_succ_lt_succ h⟩

def evaluateLiteral {n : Nat} (a : Bits n) (literal : Literal n) : Bool :=
  if literal.positive then readBit n a literal.index
  else !(readBit n a literal.index)

def evaluate {n : Nat} (C : CNF n) (a : Bits n) : Bool :=
  C.all (fun clause => clause.any (evaluateLiteral a))

/-- The original CNF source is passed to the direct H24 constructor. -/
def constructCNFMinimum {n : Nat} (C : CNF n) :
    Optimal (grade (verify := evaluate C) n) :=
  Hilbert24Construct.constructMinimum n (evaluate C)

theorem original_CNF_minimum {n : Nat} (C : CNF n)
    (competitor : Candidate (evaluate C)) :
    grade n (constructCNFMinimum C).witness ≤ grade n competitor :=
  (constructCNFMinimum C).least competitor

def originalCNFCertificate {n : Nat} (C : CNF n) : Certificate (evaluate C) :=
  certificateFromMinimum n (constructCNFMinimum C)

theorem original_CNF_sat_return {n : Nat} (C : CNF n) :
    answerBit (originalCNFCertificate C) = true ↔ ∃ a, evaluate C a = true :=
  answerBit_true_iff _

theorem original_CNF_unsat_return {n : Nat} (C : CNF n) :
    answerBit (originalCNFCertificate C) = false ↔ ∀ a, evaluate C a = false :=
  answerBit_false_iff _

def positive {n : Nat} (i : Fin n) : Literal n := ⟨i,true⟩
def negative {n : Nat} (i : Fin n) : Literal n := ⟨i,false⟩

def emptySource : CNF 0 := []
def emptyClause : CNF 0 := [[]]
def positiveOne : CNF 1 := [[positive ⟨0,by decide⟩]]
def contradictoryOne : CNF 1 :=
  [[positive ⟨0,by decide⟩], [negative ⟨0,by decide⟩]]
def attachmentSource : CNF 3 :=
  [[positive ⟨0,by decide⟩,positive ⟨1,by decide⟩,positive ⟨2,by decide⟩],
   [negative ⟨0,by decide⟩,negative ⟨1,by decide⟩,positive ⟨2,by decide⟩]]
def contradictoryThree : CNF 3 := attachmentSource ++
  [[positive ⟨2,by decide⟩], [negative ⟨2,by decide⟩]]

#eval grade 0 (constructCNFMinimum emptySource).witness
#eval grade 0 (constructCNFMinimum emptyClause).witness
#eval grade 1 (constructCNFMinimum positiveOne).witness
#eval grade 1 (constructCNFMinimum contradictoryOne).witness
#eval grade 3 (constructCNFMinimum attachmentSource).witness
#eval grade 3 (constructCNFMinimum contradictoryThree).witness
#eval returnedBits 3 (evaluate attachmentSource)
#eval answerBit (originalCNFCertificate contradictoryThree)

#print axioms constructCNFMinimum
#print axioms original_CNF_minimum
#print axioms originalCNFCertificate
#print axioms original_CNF_sat_return
#print axioms original_CNF_unsat_return

end Hilbert24CNF
Portable original calibration CASL and explicit induction obligations

Focused_Original_Proofs/Hilbert24_Portable.het · SHA-256 1b20ad508474691eb87aa48e45ba53a9b7970b03029b11e905c488b8fea7fea1

library Hilbert24_Portable
logic CASL

%% Extracted unchanged specifications from the original programme source:
%% Final_Proofs_Seven_Twenty_GRH_YM_Hilbert24_PNP.het.
%% Ordinary equations below define the admitted algebra/calibration interface.
%% Every %implied formula remains a proof obligation.
%% The induction lemmas added at the end expose their hypotheses explicitly.
%% Automated first-order replay does not assert a classical or constructive
%% metatheory for the entire institution; the companion gives structural proofs.

spec OrderedChargeGroup =
  sort Charge
  op zero : Charge
  op plus : Charge * Charge -> Charge
  op minus : Charge -> Charge
  pred nonnegative : Charge
  vars a,b,c : Charge
  . plus(plus(a,b),c) = plus(a,plus(b,c))
    %(charge_assoc)%
  . plus(a,b) = plus(b,a) %(charge_comm)%
  . plus(a,zero) = a %(charge_zero)%
  . plus(a,minus(a)) = zero %(charge_inverse)%
  . nonnegative(zero) %(charge_zero_nonnegative)%
  . nonnegative(a) /\ nonnegative(b) => nonnegative(plus(a,b))
    %(charge_nonnegative_stable)%
  . nonnegative(a) /\ nonnegative(b) /\ plus(a,b) = zero => a = zero /\ b = zero
    %(charge_nonnegative_no_cancellation)%
end


spec CalibrationSteps = OrderedChargeGroup then
  sort Judgement
  op potential : Judgement -> Charge
  op localExcess : Charge * Charge * Judgement -> Charge
  free type PremiseSlots ::= emptySlots | consSlot(Judgement; PremiseSlots)
  op premisePotential : PremiseSlots -> Charge
  vars q,p : Judgement; w,a,b,c : Charge; slots : PremiseSlots
  . localExcess(w,a,q) = plus(plus(w,a),minus(potential(q)))
    %(local_excess_def)%
  . premisePotential(emptySlots) = zero %(premise_potential_empty)%
  . premisePotential(consSlot(p,slots)) = plus(potential(p),premisePotential(slots))
    %(premise_potential_cons)%
end


spec HCProofData = CalibrationSteps then
  free types HCProof ::= hcStep(hcConclusion:Judgement;hcCharge:Charge;hcChildren:HCForest);
             HCForest ::= hcEmpty | hcCons(HCProof;HCForest)
  ops hcPremises : HCForest -> PremiseSlots;
      hcWork,hcExcess : HCProof -> Charge;
      hcForestWork,hcForestExcess : HCForest -> Charge
  preds hcRule : Charge * PremiseSlots * Judgement;
        hcValid,hcTight : HCProof;
        hcForestValid,hcForestTight : HCForest;
        hcNoMore : Charge * Charge
  forall p : HCProof; ps : HCForest; q : Judgement; w,a,b : Charge
  . hcPremises(hcEmpty)=emptySlots
  . hcPremises(hcCons(p,ps))=consSlot(hcConclusion(p),hcPremises(ps))
  . hcForestWork(hcEmpty)=zero
  . hcForestWork(hcCons(p,ps))=plus(hcWork(p),hcForestWork(ps))
  . hcWork(hcStep(q,w,ps))=plus(w,hcForestWork(ps))
  . hcForestExcess(hcEmpty)=zero
  . hcForestExcess(hcCons(p,ps))=plus(hcExcess(p),hcForestExcess(ps))
  . hcExcess(hcStep(q,w,ps))=
      plus(localExcess(w,premisePotential(hcPremises(ps)),q),hcForestExcess(ps))
  . hcForestValid(hcEmpty)
  . hcForestValid(hcCons(p,ps)) <=> hcValid(p) /\ hcForestValid(ps)
  . hcValid(hcStep(q,w,ps)) <=> hcRule(w,hcPremises(ps),q) /\ hcForestValid(ps)
  . hcForestTight(hcEmpty)
  . hcForestTight(hcCons(p,ps)) <=> hcTight(p) /\ hcForestTight(ps)
  . hcTight(hcStep(q,w,ps)) <=>
      localExcess(w,premisePotential(hcPremises(ps)),q)=zero /\ hcForestTight(ps)
  . hcNoMore(a,b) <=> nonnegative(plus(b,minus(a)))
  . hcRule(w,hcPremises(ps),q) =>
      nonnegative(localExcess(w,premisePotential(hcPremises(ps)),q))
      %(hc_calibration_of_each_actual_admitted_rule)%
end


spec HCWholeProofReturns = HCProofData then
  forall p : HCProof; ps : HCForest
  . hcForestExcess(ps)=plus(hcForestWork(ps),minus(premisePotential(hcPremises(ps))))
      %(hc_all_premise_occurrences_telescope)% %implied
  . hcExcess(p)=plus(hcWork(p),minus(potential(hcConclusion(p))))
      %(hc_entire_original_proof_excess_identity)% %implied
  . hcForestValid(ps) => nonnegative(hcForestExcess(ps))
      %(hc_forest_calibration_nonnegative)% %implied
  . hcValid(p) => nonnegative(hcExcess(p))
      %(hc_every_admitted_original_proof_has_nonnegative_excess)% %implied
  . hcValid(p) => hcNoMore(potential(hcConclusion(p)),hcWork(p))
      %(hc_original_conclusion_potential_bounds_all_proofs)% %implied
  . hcValid(p) => (hcExcess(p)=zero <=> hcTight(p))
      %(hc_zero_excess_iff_all_local_inequalities_tight)% %implied
end


spec HCMinimumReturn = HCWholeProofReturns then
  pred hcMinimum : HCProof
  forall p : HCProof
  . hcMinimum(p) <=> hcValid(p) /\
      (forall q : HCProof . hcValid(q) /\ hcConclusion(q)=hcConclusion(p) =>
         hcNoMore(hcWork(p),hcWork(q)))
  . hcValid(p) /\ hcExcess(p)=zero => hcMinimum(p)
      %(hc_constructed_matching_bound_proves_full_original_minimum)% %implied
  . hcValid(p) /\ hcTight(p) => hcMinimum(p)
      %(hc_locally_tight_proof_is_globally_minimum)% %implied
end


spec HCNativeProofData = HCMinimumReturn then
  sort HCNativeProof
  ops hcEncodeProof : HCProof -> HCNativeProof;
      hcDecodeProof : HCNativeProof -> HCProof;
      hcNativeWork : HCNativeProof -> Charge;
      hcNativeConclusion : HCNativeProof -> Judgement
  preds hcNativeValid,hcNativeMinimum : HCNativeProof
  forall p : HCProof; x : HCNativeProof
  . hcDecodeProof(hcEncodeProof(p))=p
  . hcEncodeProof(hcDecodeProof(x))=x
  . hcNativeWork(x)=hcWork(hcDecodeProof(x))
  . hcNativeConclusion(x)=hcConclusion(hcDecodeProof(x))
  . hcNativeValid(x) <=> hcValid(hcDecodeProof(x))
  . hcNativeMinimum(x) <=> hcNativeValid(x) /\
      (forall y : HCNativeProof . hcNativeValid(y) /\
        hcNativeConclusion(y)=hcNativeConclusion(x) =>
          hcNoMore(hcNativeWork(x),hcNativeWork(y)))
end


spec HCNativeMinimumReturns = HCNativeProofData then
  forall p : HCProof; x : HCNativeProof
  . hcNativeMinimum(hcEncodeProof(p)) <=> hcMinimum(p)
      %(hc_minimum_returns_through_whole_proof_chart)% %implied
  . hcValid(p) /\ hcExcess(p)=zero => hcNativeMinimum(hcEncodeProof(p))
      %(hc_calibration_certificate_transports_to_native_proof)% %implied
  . hcDecodeProof(x)=p =>
      hcNativeConclusion(x)=hcConclusion(p) /\ hcNativeWork(x)=hcWork(p)
      %(hc_same_judgement_and_complete_charge_return)% %implied
end


%% Constructor steps for simultaneous structural induction on HCProof/HCForest.
%% Each induction hypothesis is an antecedent, never a global axiom.
spec HCInductionSteps = HCProofData then
  forall p : HCProof; ps : HCForest; q : Judgement; w : Charge
  . hcForestExcess(hcEmpty)=plus(hcForestWork(hcEmpty),minus(premisePotential(hcPremises(hcEmpty))))
      %(h24_excess_empty)% %implied
  . hcExcess(p)=plus(hcWork(p),minus(potential(hcConclusion(p)))) /\
    hcForestExcess(ps)=plus(hcForestWork(ps),minus(premisePotential(hcPremises(ps)))) =>
    hcForestExcess(hcCons(p,ps))=plus(hcForestWork(hcCons(p,ps)),minus(premisePotential(hcPremises(hcCons(p,ps)))))
      %(h24_excess_cons_step)% %implied
  . hcForestExcess(ps)=plus(hcForestWork(ps),minus(premisePotential(hcPremises(ps)))) =>
    hcExcess(hcStep(q,w,ps))=plus(hcWork(hcStep(q,w,ps)),minus(potential(hcConclusion(hcStep(q,w,ps)))))
      %(h24_excess_tree_step)% %implied
  . nonnegative(hcForestExcess(hcEmpty))
      %(h24_nonnegative_empty)% %implied
  . (hcValid(p) => nonnegative(hcExcess(p))) /\
    (hcForestValid(ps) => nonnegative(hcForestExcess(ps))) =>
    (hcForestValid(hcCons(p,ps)) => nonnegative(hcForestExcess(hcCons(p,ps))))
      %(h24_nonnegative_cons_step)% %implied
  . (hcForestValid(ps) => nonnegative(hcForestExcess(ps))) =>
    (hcValid(hcStep(q,w,ps)) => nonnegative(hcExcess(hcStep(q,w,ps))))
      %(h24_nonnegative_tree_step)% %implied
  . hcForestExcess(hcEmpty)=zero <=> hcForestTight(hcEmpty)
      %(h24_tight_empty)% %implied
  . nonnegative(hcExcess(p)) /\ nonnegative(hcForestExcess(ps)) /\
    (hcExcess(p)=zero <=> hcTight(p)) /\
    (hcForestExcess(ps)=zero <=> hcForestTight(ps)) =>
    (hcForestExcess(hcCons(p,ps))=zero <=> hcForestTight(hcCons(p,ps)))
      %(h24_tight_cons_step)% %implied
  . hcRule(w,hcPremises(ps),q) /\ nonnegative(hcForestExcess(ps)) /\
    (hcForestExcess(ps)=zero <=> hcForestTight(ps)) =>
    (hcExcess(hcStep(q,w,ps))=zero <=> hcTight(hcStep(q,w,ps)))
      %(h24_tight_tree_step)% %implied
  . hcExcess(p)=plus(hcWork(p),minus(potential(hcConclusion(p)))) /\
    nonnegative(hcExcess(p)) => hcNoMore(potential(hcConclusion(p)),hcWork(p))
      %(h24_calibration_lower_bound_step)% %implied
end

%% Definition-only replay nodes avoid importing unproved %implied goals.
spec HCMinimumDefinitions = HCProofData then
  pred hcMinimum : HCProof
  forall p : HCProof
  . hcMinimum(p) <=> hcValid(p) /\
      (forall q : HCProof . hcValid(q) /\ hcConclusion(q)=hcConclusion(p) =>
         hcNoMore(hcWork(p),hcWork(q)))
end

spec HCNativeDefinitions = HCMinimumDefinitions then
  sort HCNativeProof
  ops hcEncodeProof : HCProof -> HCNativeProof;
      hcDecodeProof : HCNativeProof -> HCProof;
      hcNativeWork : HCNativeProof -> Charge;
      hcNativeConclusion : HCNativeProof -> Judgement
  preds hcNativeValid,hcNativeMinimum : HCNativeProof
  forall p : HCProof; x : HCNativeProof
  . hcDecodeProof(hcEncodeProof(p))=p
  . hcEncodeProof(hcDecodeProof(x))=x
  . hcNativeWork(x)=hcWork(hcDecodeProof(x))
  . hcNativeConclusion(x)=hcConclusion(hcDecodeProof(x))
  . hcNativeValid(x) <=> hcValid(hcDecodeProof(x))
  . hcNativeMinimum(x) <=> hcNativeValid(x) /\
      (forall y : HCNativeProof . hcNativeValid(y) /\
        hcNativeConclusion(y)=hcNativeConclusion(x) =>
          hcNoMore(hcNativeWork(x),hcNativeWork(y)))
end




spec HCNativeReplayReturns = HCNativeDefinitions then
  forall p : HCProof; x : HCNativeProof
  . hcNativeMinimum(hcEncodeProof(p)) <=> hcMinimum(p)
      %(h24_native_minimum_iff)% %implied
  . hcDecodeProof(x)=p =>
      hcNativeConclusion(x)=hcConclusion(p) /\ hcNativeWork(x)=hcWork(p)
      %(h24_native_original_grade)% %implied
end

%% The global comparison is explicit; all competitors retain the original type.
spec HCMinimumComposition = HCMinimumDefinitions then
  forall p : HCProof
  . hcValid(p) /\ hcWork(p)=potential(hcConclusion(p)) /\
    (forall q : HCProof . hcValid(q) /\ hcConclusion(q)=hcConclusion(p) =>
      hcNoMore(potential(hcConclusion(q)),hcWork(q))) => hcMinimum(p)
      %(h24_attained_bound_composes_to_minimum)% %implied
  . hcExcess(p)=plus(hcWork(p),minus(potential(hcConclusion(p)))) /\
    hcExcess(p)=zero => hcWork(p)=potential(hcConclusion(p))
      %(h24_zero_excess_attains_potential)% %implied
end

%% Pure charge identities used as independently checked local algebra lemmas.
spec H24ChargeLemmas = OrderedChargeGroup then
  forall a,b,c,d : Charge
  . minus(plus(a,b))=plus(minus(a),minus(b))
      %(h24_charge_inverse_sum)% %implied
  . plus(plus(a,minus(b)),plus(c,minus(d)))=
      plus(plus(a,c),minus(plus(b,d)))
      %(h24_charge_pair_telescopes)% %implied
  . plus(plus(plus(a,b),minus(c)),plus(d,minus(b)))=
      plus(plus(a,d),minus(c))
      %(h24_charge_internal_boundary_cancels)% %implied
end



spec H24LocalChargeLemma = CalibrationSteps and H24ChargeLemmas then
  forall w,a,b : Charge; q : Judgement
  . plus(localExcess(w,a,q),plus(b,minus(a)))=
      plus(plus(w,b),minus(potential(q)))
      %(h24_local_charge_cancellation)% %implied
end

%% These two local steps use only the independently replayed charge lemmas.
%% Their source statements are identical to the corresponding induction steps.
spec HCExcessWithCheckedCharge = HCProofData and H24LocalChargeLemma then
  forall p : HCProof; ps : HCForest; q : Judgement; w : Charge
  . hcExcess(p)=plus(hcWork(p),minus(potential(hcConclusion(p)))) /\
    hcForestExcess(ps)=plus(hcForestWork(ps),minus(premisePotential(hcPremises(ps)))) =>
    hcForestExcess(hcCons(p,ps))=plus(hcForestWork(hcCons(p,ps)),minus(premisePotential(hcPremises(hcCons(p,ps)))))
      %(h24_excess_cons_step)% %implied
  . hcForestExcess(ps)=plus(hcForestWork(ps),minus(premisePotential(hcPremises(ps)))) =>
    hcExcess(hcStep(q,w,ps))=plus(hcWork(hcStep(q,w,ps)),minus(potential(hcConclusion(hcStep(q,w,ps)))))
      %(h24_excess_tree_step)% %implied
end
